Those cards were not marked `interactive`, so clicking the visually dominant surface emitted nothing; with zero points the only other buttons were disabled without an explanatory selected state (`pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` council template/styles; `components/empires-endgame/EmpireCard.vue`).
- **Fix direction:** scope action-button CSS to the action row, make council cards selectable, and show why actions are unavailable; cover contrast/selection in browser tests.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — only direct `.council-actions` buttons receive the dark action style; every retained card is interactive/selectable and the zero-point state explains how points are earned. Non-interactive cards elsewhere render as articles instead of fake buttons. Cypress checks computed card luminance, selection and a real point-spending improvement (`EmpiresEndgame.vue` Council template/styles; `EmpireCard.vue`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M57. Technology coordinates are interpreted as percentages, pushing nearly the whole tree off-canvas
- **Expected:** all configured doctrine/technology nodes are reachable on a scrollable tree; constructor mode permits dragging and relinking them.
- **Actual before the fix:** the default definition stores authored canvas coordinates such as x=180..900 and y=40..1,360, but `TechTree` appended `%` and clipped overflow in a fixed 600px canvas. Only the x=20/y=40 root remained visible; the other nodes and their edges were off-screen, and therefore could not be selected or dragged (`public/empires-endgame/game-config.json` technology `position`; `components/empires-endgame/TechTree.vue`).
- **Fix direction:** render authored coordinates on a sized, scrollable canvas and keep drag emissions in the same coordinate space; browser-test node reachability, selection and editor dragging.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — `TechTree` now derives a world-pixel canvas from all configured extents and exposes scrollbar, background-pan, touch and keyboard navigation. Nodes/edges share one origin; constructor drag accounts for scroll and grab offset before emitting integer world coordinates. The detail panel also exposes full costs and blocking dependencies. Cypress reaches all 62 configured nodes, selects the last one and verifies an editor drag changes its position (`TechTree.vue`; `EmpiresEndgame.vue` `technologyNodes`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M58. Hand-card flag passives stack permanently after cards leave the hand
- **Expected:** only cards currently held during an empire phase contribute their normal/inverted passive flags.
- **Actual before the fix:** `startEmpirePhase` included `flag` in card phase effects, and `applyEffects` added those values directly to persistent `state.empire.flags`. No transition removed or rebuilt the contribution, so repeated cons accumulated ghost bonuses and penalties from cards no longer held (`features/empires-endgame/engine.ts` `startEmpirePhase`/`applyEffects`).
- **Fix direction:** serialize the current hand-card flag contribution separately, subtract it when the empire/event phase ends and before rebuilding the next one, and preserve permanent flags from gifts, events and research.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — serialized `cardFlagBonuses` records only the current hand's contribution; the engine subtracts it before rebuilding a card empire and when leaving empire/event flow, without touching permanent flags. Legacy empire/event snapshots reconstruct active contribution ownership from their held card faces; other phases default it empty. Focused tests prove temporary and permanent values separate correctly (`engine.ts` `heldCardFlagBonuses`/`recordCardFlagBonuses`/`clearCardFlagBonuses`/`validateAndCloneSnapshot`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M59. The table exposes the next draw as the trump card
- **Expected:** the visible bottom card supplies the trump suit and remains the last card drawn.
- **Actual before the fix:** the engine draws with `deck.pop()` and derives trump from the bottom/index-0 side, but the page rendered `deck.at(-1)`. The UI therefore exposed the next draw and could show a suit unrelated to the real trump (`features/empires-endgame/engine.ts` `drawToHand`/`resolveTrumpSuit`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` `trumpCardView`).
- **Fix direction:** render `deck[0]` and assert the preview matches the engine's trump in deterministic tests.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — `trumpCardView` renders `deck[0]`; the deterministic deck inspector/test separately identifies index 0 as the trump source and the final element as the next `pop()` draw (`EmpiresEndgame.vue` `trumpCardView`; `qa.ts` `inspectEmpiresQaDeck`; `qa.spec.ts`).

### M60. Legacy empire saves cannot release card flags already folded into persistent state
- **Expected:** a schema-v1 save made before M58 still removes its active held-card flags when the restored empire/event phase ends.
- **Actual before the fix:** the migration filled a missing `cardFlagBonuses` field with `{}`. Those old saves already contained the current hand's flag values in `empire.flags`, so the later cleanup had no ownership record to subtract and made the bonuses permanent (`engine.ts` pre-fix `validateAndCloneSnapshot`).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — restoration reconstructs the contribution from each held card's current side and level during empire/event phases without applying it a second time. A regression restores both phase shapes and proves event resolution returns the flag to its permanent baseline (`engine.ts` `heldCardFlagBonuses`/`validateAndCloneSnapshot`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M61. QA controls can overwrite or delete a real local campaign
- **Expected:** `?qa=1` is an isolated test stand; reseeding, importing, starting over and exercising the constructor must not touch the player's production campaign or custom definition.
- **Actual before the fix:** subscription autosave was guarded, but successful save import still wrote the production campaign, New Campaign cleared it, and constructor save/reset wrote or deleted production configuration/storage (`pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` pre-fix `importSave`/`startNewCampaign`/`saveEditor`/`resetEditor`).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — all four paths are explicitly ephemeral in QA mode, use QA-specific confirmation/status text, and constructor reset loads the bundled definition directly without clearing local storage. Cypress plants byte-identical campaign/config sentinels, executes every mutation including a valid import, and verifies both survive (`config.ts` `loadBundledEmpiresConfig`; `EmpiresEndgame.vue`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M62. Recruited soldiers remain in the civilian population and workforce
- **Expected:** every unit's population cost consumes ready military population and the corresponding people/classes from the city; food safety must reflect the smaller workforce and the new army.
- **Actual before the fix:** recruitment decremented only `militaryPopulation`. Total/class population, civilian food demand and available workers stayed unchanged while army upkeep was added, counting the same people on both sides of the economy (`engine.ts` pre-fix `recruitUnits`).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — recruitment proportionally consumes only configured `canRecruit` classes (with a classless fallback), total population and the ready pool. Validation projects the post-recruit population, operational-building shutdowns, production, civilian consumption and army upkeep before spending resources. Focused tests cover exact class deductions and a farm that becomes food-unsafe after its workers enlist (`engine.ts` `consumeRecruitmentPopulation`/`recruitUnits`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M63. The browser harness can run Cypress against a stale server
- **Expected:** the self-starting QA command proves that the Vite process it launched owns the requested port before browser tests begin.
- **Actual before the fix:** readiness checked `curl` before child liveness. If another process already answered on the port, the request succeeded while strict-port Vite was still starting and about to exit, so Cypress targeted the unrelated service (`tools/test-empires-endgame.sh` pre-fix readiness loop).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — readiness now requires Vite's own `Local:` marker, a successful request and a live launched PID. An occupied-port reproduction exits 1 with Vite's strict-port error instead of starting Cypress (`tools/test-empires-endgame.sh`).

### M64. Position-less custom technologies collapse on the pixel tree
- **Expected:** `position` remains optional in a valid definition; omitted nodes receive reachable, non-overlapping world-pixel coordinates.
- **Actual before the fix:** fallback values retained the former percentage-scale spacing (roughly 10–100) after the tree moved to pixels, so custom nodes overlapped one another and authored nodes (`pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` pre-fix `technologyNodes`; `types.ts` optional `position`).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-14 — missing nodes use a 210×88-pixel tier grid beginning 250 pixels to the right of the greatest authored X coordinate. A mixed 62-node browser fixture removes half the positions and pairwise-checks every rendered node rectangle (`EmpiresEndgame.vue` `technologyNodes`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

## Production runtime errors (2026-07-15)

### M65. A partial fight-log row crashes every Discord fight-page render
- **Expected:** a timed DM refresh tolerates any current log snapshot; only a complete fight row containing both `<:war:...>` and `⟶` is reordered, while an incomplete/malformed row is displayed unchanged.
- **Actual before the fix:** `HandleIsNewPlayerDescription` checked that the war marker produced two combatant parts, then unconditionally read `fightLineSplit[1]`. A row such as `attacker <:war:...> defender` therefore threw IndexOutOfRangeException; continuing the inner player loop after removing a completed row was a sibling out-of-range risk. Production Game #5, round 10 stopped updating its Discord page from the 30-second timer (`GameUpdateMess.cs:1075-1136`; stack at pre-fix line 1106).
- **Failure scenario:** the renderer observes or retains a fight header before its result suffix is appended → `FightPage` throws before the embed can be sent, and every later refresh seeing that row repeats the failure.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — fight-row normalization now requires both parsed separators and breaks immediately after consuming a complete row. Partial rows remain in their original position and the rest of `FightPage` renders (`GameUpdateMess.HandleIsNewPlayerDescription`).

### M66. Gordon Auto Move can stop after one rejected fallback target and receive an illegal Block
- **Expected:** because Halflife 3 permanently replaces Gordon's Block, bot/Auto Move may announce it once; afterward a Block decision must try legal attacks and Skip only when no enemy can be attacked.
- **Actual before the fix:** Gordon's `HandleAttack(..., -10)` adapter selected only the first living, non-team candidate and returned that attack's rejection. On round 10 a first candidate such as banned Тигр was absent from the fair AI target pool but still selected by this separate fallback. `HandleFairBotAttack` then returned as though the Block choice succeeded, leaving no attack, Block or Skip; the final CRIT guard logged `didn't do anything and auto move didn't as well` and assigned a real Block, contradicting Halflife 3 (`GameReactions.cs:746-782`; `BotsBehavior.cs:3590-3639`; `CheckIfReady.cs:1507-1520`).
- **Failure scenario:** round-10 Gordon in first place uses Auto Move while the first fallback candidate is Тигр with the public ban → fair AI elects defense, the sole fallback attack is rejected, and Gordon receives the generic emergency Block.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — the Gordon adapter now tries fallback candidates in leaderboard order until the authoritative attack handler accepts one. Only exhaustion clears the queue and commits Skip; no path from the missing Block action can leave Gordon actionless or blocked (`GameReactions.HandleAttack`).

## 99 Last Chances browser-game triage (2026-07-15)

### M67. `holdThenDoubleTap` required three presses and exposed no live timing feedback
- **Expected:** the GDD/Discord gesture «Задержка + двойное нажатие» is one hold/release followed by one timely repeat tap — two presses total — and the HUD shows whether the player is holding, inside the repeat window or completing the follow-up.
- **Actual before the fix:** after the qualifying hold, the recognizer treated the first repeat as an intermediate step and waited for a second short tap, requiring three presses total. The HUD showed only cooldowns and the eventual attack, so a missed window looked identical to an ignored input (`features/last-chances/gestures.ts` `LastChancesGestureRecognizer`; `components/last-chances/WeaponCooldowns.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** hold and release either attack button, then tap once inside the configured window → no `holdThenDoubleTap` attack fires and there is no visible indication that another press is being awaited.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — one timely short press after the hold now resolves `holdThenDoubleTap`; expiry still falls back to the ordinary `hold`. Per-hand recognizer snapshots expose press, double-tap and hold-follow-up phases with remaining-time/progress feedback in the combat HUD, and focused tests cover completion, expiry and timing boundaries (`gestures.ts` `LastChancesGestureRecognizer`; `WeaponCooldowns.vue`; `gestures.spec.ts`).

### M68. 99LC was not operable from a Bluetooth DualSense
- **Expected:** a controller-only player can choose a route and play with the GDD bindings Square = left attack and Cross = right attack; standard and raw Bluetooth Sony reports are normalized and connection state is visible.
- **Actual before the fix:** the opening route map was pointer-only, the defaults were buttons 0/1 (Cross/Circle in the standard mapping), and the engine read only the first connected pad through fixed standard axes/buttons. Browsers exposing an unmapped Sony report use different face-button ordering and a different right-stick axis, while the UI exposed no connected pad, mapping profile or diagnostic state (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` pre-fix `pollGamepad`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `components/last-chances/RunMapOverlay.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** connect a DualSense over Bluetooth and open `/99lc` without a mouse → no controller action enters the first room; after pointer entry, Square/Cross may trigger the wrong hand or no useful action.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — a testable gamepad adapter arbitrates the active pad, handles standard reports plus known raw Sony/DualSense face-button and right-stick layouts, and publishes connection/profile status. Defaults are Square/button 2 for the left hand and Cross/button 0 for the right; controller navigation selects and enters available route nodes, then combat feeds the normalized buttons into the same gesture recognizers as pointer/touch input (`features/last-chances/gamepad.ts` `createLastChancesGamepadAdapter`; `engine.ts` `pollGamepad`; `RunMapOverlay.vue`; `gamepad.spec.ts`).

### M69. 99LC's basic attack is a cooldown action, not the persistent tap combo in the mechanics GDD
- **Expected:** each hand's ordinary tap has no cooldown and continues its basic combo when the same button is tapped again inside the weapon's timing window. Using a special attack or briefly losing control must not erase a still-live chain.
- **Actual before the fix:** the recognizer emitted one generic `tap`, the engine tracked no combo step/window, and `performAttack` applied the same cooldown path used by every special. The shipped spear and knife taps had 280 ms and 210 ms cooldowns respectively (`features/last-chances/gestures.ts` pre-fix `LastChancesGestureRecognizer`; `engine.ts` pre-fix `performAttack`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`).
- **Failure scenario:** perform correctly spaced ordinary taps around another hand's skill → every accepted input repeats an untracked single strike, and a tap inside the authored cooldown is silently discarded instead of extending the basic chain.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — `tap` bypasses the special-attack cooldown path and advances an independent per-hand combo until the externally authored `input.tapComboWindowMs` expires. Other-hand specials and temporary pauses do not clear the chain; room/attempt resets do. The default spear and knife tap cooldowns are now zero and regression coverage proves continuation, expiry and special-use isolation (`engine.ts` `advanceTapCombo`/`performAttack`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M70. 99LC configuration cannot represent the GDD weapon/equipment categories
- **Expected:** the data model can author two-handed weapons (ten attacks across both inputs), ordinary one-handed weapons usable in either slot including duplicate copies, primary-only and secondary-only weapons, and hybrid weapons that use both inputs only while the secondary slot is empty.
- **Actual before the fix:** a weapon carried one fixed `left|right` hand, validation required exactly two definitions with unique hands, and the engine converted that pair directly into a hand map. No valid JSON could express a catalog, loadout choice, duplicate, both-hand attack set, slot restriction or hybrid transition (`features/last-chances/types.ts` pre-fix `LastChancesWeaponDefinition`; `config.ts` pre-fix `validateWeapons`; `engine.ts` constructor).
- **Failure scenario:** add a second dagger, a two-handed bow or a hybrid spear in the Builder JSON → validation rejects the definition or the extra weapon is not an equippable runtime choice.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — definitions are now a weapon catalog with an explicit loadout and `twoHanded`, `eitherHand`, `primaryOnly`, `secondaryOnly` and `hybrid` equip modes. Two-handed/hybrid entries provide a second five-gesture attack set; either-hand entries can be selected in either slot or duplicated by ID; hybrid resolution switches automatically when a secondary is present. Valid legacy definitions still resolve by their old `hand` fields, and the previously shipped v1 definition is repaired before validation by M77. The engine and HUD consume the same resolved loadout (`types.ts` `LastChancesWeaponDefinition`/`LastChancesLoadoutDefinition`; `equipment.ts` `resolveLastChancesLoadout`; `config.ts` `validateWeapons`; `LastChances.vue`; `engine.ts`).

### M71. Shipped 99LC enemy spawns can be embedded in solid obstacles
- **Expected:** every validated spawn is inside its room's navigable floor and outside every obstacle, so a generated enemy can turn, notice, chase and attack.
- **Actual before the fix:** room validation checked only non-negative coordinates. The default `chest-gallery` points `(730,160)`, `(720,550)` and `(425,345)` intersect authored obstacles once enemy radius is included, as do `wrong-shadow-event` points `(570,595)` and `(420,345)`; `moveCircle` then rejects movement axes and line-of-sight may intersect the containing obstacle (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `features/last-chances/config.ts` pre-fix `validateRooms`; `engine.ts` `moveCircle`/`enemyCanSeePlayer`).
- **Failure scenario:** a seeded node selects one of those spawn points → the enemy begins inside collision geometry and may remain trapped and unaware for the entire room.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — configuration validation now checks player and enemy circles against room bounds and obstacles, using the largest eligible enemy radius for each room, and rejects out-of-room obstacles. All five invalid default points and adjacent unsafe formation points were moved onto navigable floor; focused invalid-geometry fixtures cover obstacle and boundary failures (`config.ts` `validateSpawnGeometry`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M72. `graph.choicesPerNode` is exceeded while repairing route coverage
- **Expected:** every next-tier room has at least one incoming route without any current node exposing more than the configured maximum number of choices.
- **Actual before the fix:** `connectTiers` first sliced each shuffled list to the configured limit, then appended uncovered next nodes without checking remaining capacity. With the shipped limit of two, generation 1 includes nodes with three outgoing choices (`features/last-chances/plan.ts` pre-fix `connectTiers`).
- **Failure scenario:** designers set a two-choice route graph and test a generated run → some route overlays offer three rooms, so the external tuning value is not authoritative.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — generation assigns one incoming edge to every next-tier node before filling each parent only up to its authored cap. Validation rejects impossible tier/capacity combinations, and deterministic-plan tests assert both complete incoming coverage and the maximum outgoing degree (`plan.ts` `connectTiers`; `config.ts` `validateGraphCapacity`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M73. The 99LC Builder's two apply actions both reset play and one skips to generation 2
- **Expected:** Builder actions state their interruption semantics precisely; a persistence-only action leaves the active attempt untouched, while an immediate apply explicitly starts a new attempt from the edited definition. Gesture names must also describe the one post-hold repeat tap implemented by M67.
- **Actual before the fix:** both “Apply live” and “Apply & restart” destroyed/recreated the engine, clearing Chances, erosion and route progress. The restart flag then called `newGeneration()` on the fresh engine, advancing directly from generation 1 to generation 2. Builder, page and cooldown copy still labelled the two-press hold-follow-up as “Hold + double tap” (`pages/LastChances.vue` pre-fix `createEngine`/`applyBuilder`; `components/last-chances/BuilderDrawer.vue`; `WeaponCooldowns.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** tune a visual or attack value mid-room and choose “Apply live” → the attempt is discarded despite the label; choose the other action → it is also discarded but begins from an unexpectedly advanced seed.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — “Save browser override” persists the validated definition without touching the active engine, room, Chances or erosion. “Apply & start fresh generation” explicitly replaces the attempt and starts the edited definition at generation 1. English/Russian gesture copy now says one post-hold tap, and the browser regression verifies both persistence and interruption semantics (`BuilderDrawer.vue`; `LastChances.vue` `createEngine`/`applyBuilder`; `WeaponCooldowns.vue`; `cypress/e2e/last-chances.cy.ts`).

### M74. 99LC has a flat spawn-point pool instead of the prototype brief's seeded layout templates
- **Expected:** every room authors several coherent enemy-spawn layouts; generation chooses one layout randomly but stores that seeded choice in the immutable plan, and death retries reproduce it exactly.
- **Actual before the fix:** a room exposed one flat `enemySpawns` array. `makeEnemyPlan` shuffled individual points for every node and, when enemy count exceeded the pool, jittered/clamped duplicate points; there was no layout identity or way to author/select a whole arrangement (`features/last-chances/types.ts` pre-fix `LastChancesRoomTemplate`; `plan.ts` pre-fix `makeEnemyPlan`/`makeNode`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`).
- **Failure scenario:** author two intentional formations for a room and regenerate the run → the schema cannot represent either formation boundary, the plan mixes individual points instead of choosing one, and over-cap duplicates may be pushed into walls even when every authored point validates.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — schema-v2 rooms now require at least two named `spawnLayouts`; each planned node selects one layout from its seed, records `spawnLayoutId`, and places enemies only on that layout's exact authored points. Validation rejects a layout that cannot hold the maximum eligible tier count, an enemy circle outside the room or inside an obstacle, and pairwise overlap at the room's largest eligible enemy radius. Every shipped room has two safe formations, while schema-v1 `enemySpawns` remains a validated legacy fallback (`types.ts` `LastChancesSpawnLayoutDefinition`/`LastChancesPlanNode`; `plan.ts` `roomSpawnLayouts`/`makeNode`; `config.ts` `validateSpawnGeometry`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M75. 99LC's live melee/burst hitbox radius and duration controls do not affect collision
- **Expected:** the prototype's externally editable attack hitbox size and attack duration change the real collision window, so tuning them in the Builder produces a testable combat difference.
- **Actual before the fix:** melee and burst executors damaged every target immediately when the gesture resolved. Melee used only range/arc and burst used only range; both ignored `attack.radius`, while `durationMs` controlled only the drawn effect even though the client documentation described it as active time (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` pre-fix `performMelee`/`performBurst`/`addEffect`; `components/last-chances/BuilderDrawer.vue`; `docs/WEB-CLIENT.md` §12A).
- **Failure scenario:** increase a melee attack's radius and active duration in Quick Tune, then let an enemy enter the visible swing before it fades → collision is identical to the old values and the later overlap never damages it.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — melee and burst gestures create a cast-origin active area for the authored `durationMs`, test overlap throughout that window, include the attack and enemy radii in reach, and damage each target at most once. Melee also preserves its authored facing arc; burst knockback remains radial. Active areas are cleared on room completion, death, retry, generation replacement and engine destruction, and focused tests cover late entry, expiry, one-hit behavior and radius padding (`engine.ts` `startActiveArea`/`updateActiveAreas`/`applyActiveAreaHits`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M76. Meaningful enemy movement tuning remains compiled into the 99LC engine
- **Expected:** values designers are likely to tune during prototype playtests live in the external definition and Builder, including how quickly an idle enemy scans and how close it tries to stand before attacking.
- **Actual before the fix:** idle facing advanced by the literal `0.28` radians/second and chase stopped at the literal `0.72 × attackRange`; neither value existed in the enemy schema or editor despite the prototype brief's explicit no-recompile tuning contract (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` pre-fix `updateEnemies`; `types.ts` `LastChancesEnemyDefinition`; `components/last-chances/BuilderDrawer.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** a playtest shows an enemy circling too slowly or crowding too close → changing every exposed enemy value cannot reproduce the desired behavior; a TypeScript edit and SPA rebuild are required.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — every schema-v2 enemy now authors `idleTurnRadiansPerSecond` and `preferredAttackRangeRatio` in the runtime JSON; validation constrains both, Quick Tune exposes both, and `updateEnemies` consumes them directly. Schema-v1 definitions retain the old `0.28`/`0.72` defaults for compatibility (`types.ts` `LastChancesEnemyDefinition`; `config.ts` `validateEnemies`; `engine.ts` `updateEnemies`; `BuilderDrawer.vue`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M77. The schema-v2 rollout strands real schema-v1 browser overrides
- **Expected:** a browser override saved from the previously deployed 99LC prototype is migrated into a safe current-runtime definition and remains editable; compatibility coverage uses the actual old definition rather than a synthetic v1-shaped copy of new data.
- **Actual before the fix:** validation accepted schema version 1 in name but applied the new zero-tap-cooldown and spawn-geometry invariants before any migration. The previously shipped 280/210 ms tap cooldowns and five obstacle-overlapping spawn points therefore made a real saved override unloadable, while the compatibility test derived its fixture from already-safe schema-v2 data (`features/last-chances/config.ts` interim `validateAttackSet`/`validateSpawnGeometry`/`loadLastChancesConfig`; `config.spec.ts` interim schema-v1 test; previous `public/99lc/game-config.json`).
- **Failure scenario:** open `/99lc` after saving a browser override on the prior deployment → loading stops at the invalid-definition screen until the player discards all local tuning.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — `migrateLastChancesConfig` deep-clones schema-v1 input, zeros every basic-tap cooldown, and relocates the exact five unsafe points from the previously shipped rooms before strict validation; schema v2 is never rewritten. Browser load/save, direct engine construction and Builder JSON validation/import all use that migration path. The regression fixture reconstructs the prior hand-slot definition, 280/210 ms taps and exact flat spawn pools, then proves the migrated result validates without fetching or discarding the override (`config.ts` `LEGACY_SPAWN_RELOCATIONS`/`migrateLastChancesConfig`/`assertValidConfig`; `engine.ts` constructor; `BuilderDrawer.vue` `parseRaw`; `config.spec.ts` `previousShippedSchemaV1Config`).

### M78. Sector-shaped 99LC burst attacks damage targets behind the player
- **Expected:** any attack that authors an arc below 360 degrees respects that facing sector in collision, including the spear's 150-degree `Широкий толчок древком`.
- **Actual before the fix:** active-area angular filtering ran only when `kind === 'melee'`; every burst was treated as a full circle even though the schema and shipped secondary spear move authored narrower arcs (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` interim `applyActiveAreaHits`; `public/99lc/game-config.json` `Широкий толчок древком`).
- **Failure scenario:** use the forward staff shove with an enemy directly behind the player but inside its radius → the unseen rear target takes damage and knockback.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — every active area with `arcDegrees < 360` now applies the same facing-dot-product gate used by melee. This matches the existing sector renderer and a regression places equal targets in front of and behind a 150-degree burst, proving that only the forward target is damaged (`engine.ts` `applyActiveAreaHits`/`renderEffect`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M79. 99LC spawn validation allows an enemy to start on top of the player
- **Expected:** every authored formation keeps enemy circles separated from the room's player spawn by at least the sum of their validated radii.
- **Actual before the fix:** player and enemy points were each checked against bounds/obstacles and enemy points against each other, but the two collections were never compared (`features/last-chances/config.ts` interim `validateSpawnGeometry`).
- **Failure scenario:** set a layout point equal to `playerSpawn` in Builder JSON → validation succeeds and combat begins with overlapping actors.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — every legacy or named layout point is now compared with `playerSpawn` using `player.radius + largest eligible enemy radius`; overlap is rejected before a plan can be built. The focused fixture puts an enemy exactly on the player and asserts the combined-radius validation error (`config.ts` `validateSpawnGeometry`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M80. 99LC burst collision reaches full size before its visible wave
- **Expected:** a duration-based expanding burst damages a distant target when the rendered wave reaches it, so visual timing and live collision describe the same attack.
- **Actual before the fix:** `startActiveArea` tested the full `range + radius` at cast time and every later frame, while `renderEffects` drew burst radius from zero to full size across `durationMs` (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` interim `startActiveArea`/`applyActiveAreaHits`/`renderEffects`).
- **Failure scenario:** cast a long-duration burst at an enemy near its outer edge → damage lands immediately while the visible wave is still at the player's feet.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — active bursts retain their total and remaining duration, and collision reach expands by the same normalized progress as the rendered wave; a zero-duration burst remains an intentional instant full-reach attack. The regression proves an outer target is untouched at cast and mid-progress, then hit when the wave completes (`engine.ts` `startActiveArea`/`updateActiveAreas`/`applyActiveAreaHits`/`renderEffect`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M81. 99LC ignores authored pierce limits outside projectile attacks
- **Expected:** the shared `pierce` field means the number of additional targets an attack may damage, so `0` hits one target, `1` hits two, and the deliberately high values on area/dash moves permit their authored crowds.
- **Actual before the fix:** projectiles tracked `pierce + 1` remaining hits, but active melee/burst areas and dashes damaged every overlapping enemy regardless of the same field. Quick Tune exposed `pierce` for every executor and shipped non-projectile attacks authored values from 0 through 8 (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` interim `RuntimeActiveArea`/`RuntimeDash`/`performProjectile`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; `components/last-chances/BuilderDrawer.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** line up several enemies in the spear's `pierce: 0` basic slash or a low-pierce dash → all of them take damage, so editing the external value has no effect.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — dashes and active melee/burst areas now initialize `remainingHits = pierce + 1`, decrement only on a new damaged target, and stop applying damage when exhausted; exhausted areas are removed while a dash still completes its authored travel. Focused three-target tests prove a `pierce: 1` area hits two and a `pierce: 0` dash hits one without stopping movement (`engine.ts` `RuntimeDash`/`RuntimeActiveArea`/`updatePlayer`/`startActiveArea`/`applyActiveAreaHits`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M82. Schema-v1 migration can move a safe customized spawn into a customized obstacle
- **Expected:** the narrow repair for previously shipped unsafe coordinates inspects the override's current circle geometry; it moves a point only when the original is unsafe and the known replacement is safe.
- **Actual before the fix:** `migrateLastChancesConfig` matched only room ID plus exact point coordinates. A user who had moved the room's obstacles could make an old point safe or its replacement unsafe, but migration still rewrote it before validation (`features/last-chances/config.ts` interim `LEGACY_SPAWN_RELOCATIONS`/`migrateLastChancesConfig`).
- **Failure scenario:** in the old `chest-gallery`, move obstacle 1 to `{x:775,y:130,width:20,height:60}` → `(730,160)` becomes safe, yet migration changes it to `(780,160)` inside the customized obstacle and rejects the override.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-15 — migration now derives the largest enemy radius eligible for each legacy room and tests the original and proposed replacement circles against the override's current bounds, obstacles and player spawn. A known point is moved only when the original is unsafe and the replacement is safe; schema v2 remains untouched. The exact customized-obstacle reproduction keeps `(730,160)`, rejects `(780,160)`, still repairs the other unsafe shipped points, and validates (`config.ts` `eligibleEnemySpawnRadii`/`spawnFitsRoomGeometry`/`migrateLastChancesConfig`; `config.spec.ts`).

### M83. Empire's Endgame consumes city-targeted gifts without asking for a target
- **Expected:** accepting a gift whose authored effect names a selected city pauses resolution, presents only legal accessible cities, persists that pending choice in the save, and applies the resources to the chosen city's economy before the empire phase begins.
- **Actual before the fix:** `chooseGift` immediately appended the gift and called `startEmpirePhase`; `EmpiresEffect.resource` could mutate only the empire-global ledger. `gift-resource-grant` therefore gave wood and iron everywhere, left its food in a gameplay-dead global stock, and offered no city choice (`features/empires-endgame/engine.ts` `chooseGift`/`applyEffects`; `types.ts` `EmpiresEffect`/`EmpiresCityState`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` `chooseGift`).
- **Failure scenario:** accept «Ресурсы в выбранный город» while two cities have different construction needs → the phase advances immediately, both cities can spend the same global grant, and no target was ever selected.
- **Fix direction:** add a serialized pending-resolution contract, city stockpiles that participate in local construction/recruitment and starvation, an accessible target picker, deterministic save restoration, and QA/browser coverage.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `EmpiresPendingGiftResolution` keeps the accepted gift in `divineGift` until `resolvePendingTarget` receives a currently accessible city; restoration rebuilds legal targets from trusted config rather than the saved list. Resource effects enter that city's ledger, which local construction/recruitment spend before the empire reserve; city then shared empire food covers settlement deficits. The target dialog, named QA fixture and Cypress flow prove the choice persists and other city ledgers remain unchanged (`types.ts` `EmpiresPendingGiftResolution`; `engine.ts` `chooseGift`/`resolvePendingTarget`/`resourcePaymentPlan`/`finishEmpireInternal`; `TargetResolutionDialog.vue`; `qa.ts`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M84. Empire's Endgame region-sacrifice gifts leave the destroyed region fully playable
- **Expected:** «Предать Запад огню» and the equivalent North/South/East rewards permanently remove access to that region: its cities cannot produce, construct, recruit, receive targets, or provide regional services.
- **Actual before the fix:** each reward only incremented an unconsumed `destroyWest`/`destroyNorth`/`destroySouth`/`destroyEast` flag. The map, city selector, production, population totals and every empire action ignored those flags (`public/empires-endgame/game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `productionForCity`/`checkEmpireAction`/`totalPopulation`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` `mapRegionViews`/`cityViews`).
- **Failure scenario:** accept «Предать Запад огню», then open a western city and upgrade its farm → the supposedly annihilated region still produces and accepts orders.
- **Fix direction:** persist authoritative region status, enforce it in every city/production/target boundary, and render the loss explicitly. Preserve the city records for save/history because the export does not specify inventory conversion.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — region-sacrifice gifts write `destroyedRegionIds`; `isRegionAccessible`/`isCityAccessible` gate targeting, production, construction, recruitment, boosts, population totals and settlement without deleting historical city records. The map keeps a lost region inspectable, marks its cities unavailable and disables them in the city selector; schema-v1 saves reconstruct the state from old sacrifice flags or claimed gifts (`engine.ts` `applyFixedGiftResolution`/`isRegionAccessibleInState`/`migrateDestroyedRegions`; `EmpireMap.vue`; `EmpiresEndgame.vue`; `engine.spec.ts`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M85. The farm/lumber relic records flags but changes neither current nor maximum building level
- **Expected:** «Реликвия плодородия и леса» raises every placed farm and lumbermill by one effective current level and raises their effective maximum by one, including production and workforce demand.
- **Actual before the fix:** the gift only added `farmLevelBonus` and `lumberLevelBonus`; building lookup, production, worker shutdown, upgrade availability and UI maximums read the unmodified configured/raw levels (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `upgradeBuilding`/`productionForCity`/`workerDemand`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue` `cityViews`).
- **Failure scenario:** accept the relic with a level-3 farm and level-2 lumbermill → both still display and produce at 3/3 and 2/3.
- **Fix direction:** make the level bonus an executable domain effect, use exact configured next levels when present and deterministic extrapolation above the authored cap, and test both effective production and UI levels.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — the relic writes typed `buildingLevelBonuses` for farm/lumber slots. `effectiveBuildingLevel` and `effectiveBuildingMaxLevel` feed production, worker demand, dependencies and the city display; `buildingLevelDefinitionAt` uses an authored level when present and deterministically extrapolates the last per-level delta beyond the catalog. Raw purchased levels remain intact for save/editor ownership, and migration reconstructs the bonus from old flags or gift history (`types.ts` `EmpiresGiftResolution`; `engine.ts` `applyFixedGiftResolution`/`effectiveBuildingLevel`/`buildingLevelDefinitionAt`/`migrateBuildingLevelBonuses`; `CityView.vue`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M86. The meteor reward has neither target selection nor a meteor impact
- **Expected:** accepting the meteor pauses for a legal city/territory target; resolving it consumes the reward once, applies a deterministic configurable impact, grants self-target meteorite iron/radiation consequences, and then continues the campaign.
- **Actual before the fix:** `gift-meteor` only incremented the unconsumed `meteorCharges` flag. There was no target state, action, charge consumption, building/city damage, insurance hook or radiation consequence (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `chooseGift`/`applyEffects`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** accept «Метеорит» → the empire phase starts with `meteorCharges: 1`, but no control ever lets the player choose a city or use it.
- **Fix direction:** implement a persisted city-target resolution. The authoritative export does not specify meteor damage, so keep the damage amount configurable; the bundled default may use the older non-conflicting note's one-building-level impact rather than hard-code an undocumented universal rule.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — the meteor uses the same persisted accessible-city target contract, then `damageCityWithMeteor` deterministically selects the highest raw-level building (ID tie-break), removes the configured `damageLevels`, and locks that building for the current con. Its iron enters only the selected city. The bundled value is the configurable one-level impact from the older non-conflicting note, not a new universal rule; the separate meteorite-iron/radiation outcome stays explicitly deferred because the export does not define an executable radiation lifecycle (`types.ts` `EmpiresGiftResolution`; `game-config.json` `gift-meteor`/`gift-meteor-iron`; `engine.ts` `applyTargetedGiftResolution`/`damageCityWithMeteor`; `TargetResolutionDialog.vue`; `engine.spec.ts`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M87. Reversed military recruiters omit the authored unit loss, downgrade and one-turn arson lock
- **Expected:** when reversed «Зазывалы в войска» is held, every accessible city loses one deterministic random unit, its military building loses one level, that building cannot be interacted with or repaired for the phase, and recruitment is disabled.
- **Actual before the fix:** only `recruitmentDisabled` had a consumer. `militaryArson` was stored as a temporary flag but never removed a unit, damaged a barracks or blocked building interaction (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `startEmpirePhase`/`recruitUnits`/`upgradeBuilding`).
- **Failure scenario:** enter an empire phase holding the reversed card in a city with recruited units and a level-3 barracks → recruitment is blocked, but all units remain and the barracks stays level 3 and upgradeable.
- **Fix direction:** resolve the city effects once at empire start with the seeded RNG, persist the downgrade and phase lock, and migrate old saves with no locks.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — the executor now applies `militaryArson` once to every accessible city: the serialized RNG chooses one present unit kind and removes one unit, the highest barracks loses one raw level, and `buildingInteractionLocks` blocks placement, repair/upgrade and production-boost interaction for that con while `recruitmentDisabled` blocks orders globally. Snapshot migration initializes locks and reconstructs the once-only transition for affected saves. Because the bundled army, Barracks and recruitment payoff are themselves outside the playable campaign, both Hearts-7 faces are explicitly deferred there instead of advertising an effect on disabled content; constructor scenarios that make those systems live use the completed executor (`engine.ts` `startEmpirePhase`/`applyMilitaryArsonToState`/`isBuildingInteractionLocked`/`validateAndCloneSnapshot`; `game-config.json` `card-hearts-7`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M88. Empire's Endgame progression unlocks and per-branch research limits are not enforced
- **Expected:** Fair, Temple, Smithy and Bank construction require their authored unlock technologies; a city may use the relic exemptions for Smithy iron and Stable livestock; each empire phase permits at most one technology/steel node per branch and one reform/doctrine per branch.
- **Actual before the fix:** the first levels of those buildings omitted their unlock prerequisites, the exemption flags had no consumer, and `research` allowed any number of nodes from the same `groupId` in one phase as long as time/resources remained (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `placeBuilding`/`checkEmpireAction`/`research`).
- **Failure scenario:** place a Fair before «Зарождение торговли», build a Smithy without «Изделия из железа», or research two Science technologies in the same empire phase → every action succeeds.
- **Fix direction:** repair the bundled dependencies, consume the two construction-exemption flags narrowly, serialize per-phase branch usage, and expose the limit in availability/error text.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — the bundled Fair, Temple, Smithy and Bank level-one definitions carry their technology dependencies. The engine consumes Smithy iron and Stable livestock exemptions only in their matching construction checks/costs; the bundled exemption relic is deferred together with those two deferred facilities, so it cannot be drafted as a current-campaign no-op. `researchUsage` serializes one technology/steel selection per `groupId` and one reform/doctrine selection per group for the current empire phase, resets at the next phase, and is mirrored in Development availability text. `giftIsUnlocked` keeps relic-kind rewards out of the draft until `relicsUnlocked`, and validation requires three executable non-relic gifts for the pre-unlock pool (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `buildingDependencies`/`buildingResourceCosts`/`firstMissingDependency`/`researchUsageKey`/`research`/`giftIsUnlocked`; `EmpiresEndgame.vue` `researchUsageKey`; `config.spec.ts`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M89. Empire's Endgame advertises purchasable mechanics that only store unread flags
- **Expected:** every card face, gift, event choice, building, technology and unit offered by the current campaign either has an executable effect or clearly states that its required system is outside the playable ruleset and cannot consume the player's resources/time.
- **Actual before the fix:** the bundled definition produces more than one hundred distinct flags, while only a handful have engine readers. Numerous technologies and buildings can spend days, food and resources for no outcome; several event choices do nothing at all; many authored card faces promise loyalty, trade, epidemic, combat, fleet or expedition effects that the runtime cannot represent. The same problem affects recruitable units: they consume population and food but have no combat or expedition payoff (`public/empires-endgame/game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `applyEffects`; `pages/EmpiresEndgame.vue`).
- **Failure scenario:** research «Компас», construct a Bank, choose «Запереть город» during the epidemic, or recruit a military unit → the cost is paid and a flag/count changes, but no playable system ever observes the result.
- **Fix direction:** audit every active definition. Implement effects whose authoritative numbers and current-loop substrate exist; add a validated deferral contract for content that requires absent combat/fleet/epidemic/diplomacy/expedition systems or unresolved design numbers, and block it in both engine and UI instead of leaving a silent no-op.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — cards/faces, gifts, resources, buildings, units, technologies and events/choices now share an explicit `deferredReason` boundary. `validateDeferredReasons` rejects empty markers and `validateLiveEffects` rejects unsupported flag effects on non-deferred content unless the engine consumes the flag or a live prerequisite references it. Drafts and engine actions cannot spend on deferred content; cards remain playable in Durak but their future face is badged and skipped in the empire phase. The bundled combat/fleet/epidemic/diplomacy/expedition systems and export-undefined faces are labelled unavailable instead of simulated by unread flags (`types.ts`; `config.ts` `validateDeferredReasons`/`validateLiveEffects`; `engine.ts` `beginDivineGift`/`placeBuilding`/`upgradeBuilding`/`recruitUnits`/`research`/`chooseEvent`/`startEmpirePhase`; `EmpireCard.vue`; `GiftDraft.vue`; `TechTree.vue`; `CityView.vue`; `config.spec.ts`; `cypress/e2e/empires-endgame.cy.ts`).

### M90. Defined current-loop progression effects are still inert
- **Expected:** the current empire phase enforces the authored barracks recruitment cap and its Hearts-7 bypass, Conservation counters the inverted famine card, Granary levels protect/discount provisions, Trade Levy converts idle buildings and surplus food into gold, Small Temples share city construction resources with the authored transfer loss, and Treasury pays its end-of-turn saved-gold return.
- **Actual before the fix:** `equippedRecruitCapacity`, `unlimitedTavernRecruitment`, `famineYearCounter`, `famineProtectionTurns`, `provisionEfficiencyPercent`, `idleBuildingGoldBase`, `surplusFoodPerGold`, `templarTransferLossPercent` and `treasuryGoldPerSavedMillion` were stored but never read, despite all required city, food, recruitment, building, research and resource ledgers existing (`game-config.json`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** recruit beyond the barracks cap, hold reversed «Голодный год» after researching Conservation, or finish a phase with a Trade Levy and food surplus → the result is identical to not owning the relevant card/building/technology.
- **Fix direction:** implement these effects as derived current-loop rules with deterministic accounting and focused tests; treat absolute cap/level flags as authored values rather than additively stacking every building level.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — operational building effects now drive the per-city barracks cap and Hearts-7 bypass, Conservation and Granary famine counters, Granary provision efficiency, Small-Temple cross-city payment with deterministic transfer loss, and Trade-Levy idle-building/surplus-food gold; Treasury pays once from whole saved millions at settlement. Values are read from the applicable operational/effective level instead of globally adding every level definition. Bundled combat units remain deferred because their absent combat payoff is not fabricated, but the recruitment executor enforces these rules for any non-deferred unit catalog (`engine.ts` `recruitUnits`/`operationalBuildingFlagValue`/`famineFoodMultiplier`/`foodConsumptionForCity`/`resourcePaymentPlan`/`tradeLevyGoldForCity`/`applyTreasuryIncome`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M91. Random-event eligibility and effect timing make authored choices permanent, late, or repeatable
- **Expected:** event prerequisites include prior event outcomes, one-crisis modifiers affect the crisis they describe, and “stop this recurring problem” choices prevent that event from being selected again.
- **Actual before the fix:** `eventIsEligible` checks only static definition prerequisites. «Найти и казнить предводителя» sets `horseTheftDisabled` but horse theft remains eligible forever. «Строгие пайки» is offered only after starvation has already been resolved, then its `starvationLossMultiplierPercent` modifier persists into every later con instead of the current crisis. Several other events offer only future-system flag choices (`engine.ts` `finishEmpireInternal`/`eventIsEligible`/`chooseEvent`; `game-config.json`).
- **Failure scenario:** end a famine phase, choose strict rationing, then observe no benefit to that famine but a permanent 20% loss reduction thereafter; or eliminate the horse thieves and immediately roll the same theft again.
- **Fix direction:** enforce outcome-based event suppression, give temporary crisis effects an explicit lifecycle or defer the event until its settlement order is implemented, and exclude events whose choices depend entirely on absent systems.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `finishEmpireInternal` calculates `uncoveredFoodDeficit` and lets the famine event win the event roll before `settleEmpireEconomy`; `EmpiresEventState.empireSettlementPending` persists that ordering across autosave. `chooseEvent` applies the ration/purchase choice, settles the same crisis, then restores/removes the temporary starvation multiplier before the next con. `eventIsEligible` excludes deferred events/choices and suppresses horse theft after `horseTheftDisabled`; schema-v1 event saves without the marker retain their historical post-settlement meaning (`types.ts` `EmpiresEventState`; `engine.ts` `finishEmpireInternal`/`uncoveredFoodDeficit`/`settleEmpireEconomy`/`chooseEvent`/`eventIsEligible`; `engine.spec.ts`).

## 99 Last Chances authored-weapon completion (2026-07-16)

### M92. The 99LC attack schema cannot express the authored seven-weapon mechanics
- **Expected:** attacks may retain exact charge duration, select release bands, channel while held, recover/cancel, use per-skill invulnerability, repeat or follow movement/aim, and expose disabled gesture slots where the brief defines no action.
- **Actual before the fix:** every gesture resolves one fixed melee/projectile/dash/burst immediately; input emits only the gesture name, `doubleTapHold` fires at the global hold threshold, all dashes grant invulnerability, and the schema has no charge bands, channels, recovery or conditional mechanics (`features/last-chances/types.ts`; `gestures.ts`; `engine.ts` `performAttack`).
- **Failure scenario:** hold the spear for an early, medium or late release → every duration produces the same fixed projectile and the runtime cannot retain which release was authored.
- **Fix direction:** introduce a charge-aware gesture lifecycle, schema-v3 attack behavior/collider/effect metadata and reusable runtime action state.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — schema v3 now carries behavior, exact collider, ordered charge bands, recovery/root/invulnerability, repeated-hit, resource, status and live-tuning metadata. Gesture resolutions retain both press durations; the engine rejects releases below the first armed band, maintains held channels, and exposes the same action state to Canvas, touch and the accessible HUD (`types.ts` `LastChancesAttackDefinition`/`LastChancesChargeDefinition`; `gestures.ts` `LastChancesGestureRecognizer`; `weapon-runtime.ts`; `engine.ts` `performAttack`/`updateHeldWeaponMechanics`/`createSnapshot`; focused config/gesture/runtime tests).

### M93. The shipped spear is not the authored two-handed spear
- **Expected:** spear attacks have an inner dead zone and edge critical zone, charge-scaled ram, three hold-release outcomes, charged spin follow-up, real parry/push/armor stance, turning contact stance and invulnerable pole vault.
- **Actual before the fix:** `primary-spear` is a generic hybrid composed only from fixed arc/projectile/dash/burst executors; it has no distance bands, wall pin/carry, charge branches or continuous stance (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** hit an enemy point-blank or at the spear tip, or release the hold in different sectors → damage and behavior remain identical.
- **Fix direction:** replace it with the authored two-handed kit and make every distance/charge/control rule externally tunable.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `twohand-spear` supplies ten authored inputs: strict body-overlap/tip-critical basics, charge-scaled ram, early wide release, medium targeted stun throw, late piercing carry-and-wall-pin throw, charged overhead spin, short-release provisional parry, range-setting shove/charge-scaled kick, aim-following contact/slice stance and traversal-only invulnerable pole vault. A successful provisional parry consumes a queued compound gesture; an unused one is canceled by that gesture. The late kick reaches the primary critical band, while carried enemies pin only at an obstacle or perimeter and are backtracked to safe geometry. Projectile/dash offsets and strict inner colliders preserve the close dead zone, and all charge/collider/impact values remain in runtime JSON (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `twohand-spear`; `colliders.ts` strict inner exclusion; `engine.ts` `beginProvisionalTapParry`/`performSpearRelease`/`pinProjectileTargets`/`dashStepColliders`/`updateHeldWeaponMechanics`; engine/collider tests).

### M94. Chain is absent from the playable 99LC catalog
- **Expected:** Chain captures a non-bleed DOT, stores it without expiry, spreads it on the next chain attack, supports parry/disarm combo, rotation-assisted repeated spin, group throw, charged hook/drag and a seven-second bind that removes the chain until recovery.
- **Actual before the fix:** no chain definition or runtime state exists; the engine has no DOT carrier, tether, bound target, attack-rate slow or temporarily unavailable weapon (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `types.ts`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** equip the intended secondary weapon after applying poison → there is no chain to capture or redistribute the effect.
- **Fix direction:** add the secondary-only Chain plus persistent per-weapon DOT/tether/loss state.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `secondary-chain` captures the strongest poison/burn/chemical DOT without a timer and consumes it on the next chain action; its parrying/disarming combo, movement-assisted second spin, group directional throw, charge-scaled hook/drag and seven-second bind are implemented. Bind is not a hidden stun: separate authored slow and attack-slow effects leave the target mobile but attacking four times less often. Binding empties the chain resource until that target dies or a boss actually loses the configured max-HP share from one hit; that recovery also applies its authored boss stun. Resource, stored-DOT and bind/status indicators remain visible in the HUD/world (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `secondary-chain`; `statuses.ts` `captureLastChancesDot`/`spreadLastChancesDot`; `engine.ts` chain branches/resource lifecycle; focused status/engine tests).

### M95. Claws are absent from the playable 99LC catalog
- **Expected:** Claws can occupy either or both hands, have no ordinary combo, bleed on every even successful slash, reward alternating dual-hand rhythm, block healing, disarm, charge a dash and convert the follow-up window into a deep critical strike.
- **Actual before the fix:** no claw definition exists and tap sequencing is only a cyclic authored combo; the runtime has no successful-hit parity, heal block, disarm or dual-hand rhythm state (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `engine.ts` `advanceTapCombo`).
- **Failure scenario:** duplicate the intended weapon into both slots and alternate taps → no bleed parity or rhythm microstagger can occur.
- **Fix direction:** add either-hand Claws with shared successful-hit state and generic status hooks.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `either-claws` can occupy either or both slots, keeps ordinary slashes on one non-cyclic action, bleeds every second damaging claw hit and microstaggers properly timed alternating hands. Its rend blocks bleed healing, weapon catch disarms, charged dash scales a damage-free traversal and creates the damaging scratch only at its endpoint, and timed cancellation becomes a deep critical; Fire, Chemical and Poison Symbols are behavior-scoped to the authored rend, trail and bleeding-target poison interactions. The healing-block timer freezes the bleed wound timer without suppressing its damage ticks (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `either-claws`; `equipment.ts`; `statuses.ts` `updateLastChancesStatuses`; `engine.ts` `damageEnemy`/dash landing/trail branches; status/augment/engine tests).

### M96. Knife-spider cannot be captured or used as its authored living weapon
- **Expected:** after dodging its leap, a player behind the recovering Knife-spider can interact to replace the secondary weapon; the living knife loses health through use/damage, accelerates thrusts, bleeds, can be twisted deeply or destroyed by its charged throw.
- **Actual before the fix:** Knife-spider exists only as an enemy. There is no combat interaction prompt/input, capture window, weapon durability or self-damage lifecycle (`engine.ts`; `TouchControls.vue`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`).
- **Failure scenario:** dodge the leap and stand behind the spider before it turns → no interaction is offered and the secondary slot cannot become the spider knife.
- **Fix direction:** add a deterministic rear-capture interaction, ephemeral secondary weapon state and durability feedback.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — a missed locked Knife-spider leap preserves its locked facing and creates a true rear capture window with keyboard, touch and gamepad interaction feedback. Capture replaces the secondary slot with `secondary-spider-knife`; use and successful hits consume visible durability, held thrusts accelerate, twist refreshes/amplifies bleed, and the charged throw consumes all remaining durability and removes the living weapon. Enemy Knife-spiders also pay self-damage when they attack (`engine.ts` `updateEnemies`/`capturableKnifeSpider`/`captureKnifeSpider`/enemy attack paths/spider durability branches; `TouchControls.vue`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; focused engine/page tests).

### M97. The generic hammer does not implement the authored two-handed Axe
- **Expected:** every Axe hit hooks inward; skills cause recovery that an empowered tap cancels; the kit includes fourth-hit bleed, choke/armor tear or aimed throw, long parry, movement-assisted reflecting spin and an invulnerable momentum leap with landing impact.
- **Actual before the fix:** `twohand-hammer` only uses broad fixed damage/knockback actions and has no hook pull, recovery cancel, grabbed target, projectile reflection or spin inertia (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** use a special then immediately tap, or rotate movement during the held spin → neither the recovery nor number of rotations changes.
- **Fix direction:** replace the hammer with the authored Axe and reusable recovery/grab/channel rules.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `twohand-axe` replaces the hammer with hook pull on every hit, fourth-hit bleed, post-action recovery canceled by the next empowered combo tap, single-target choke/late armor tear, held target spin-and-aimed throw, long parry and held reflecting spin. Matching movement/aim rotation doubles its sampled revolutions and preserves the release tangent for the invulnerable leap; flight is non-damaging except for Chemical trail, while the small landing circle alone deals the massive damage/stun. Charged invulnerability covers the entire resolved flight rather than the unscaled base duration (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `twohand-axe`; `engine.ts` `latchAxeTarget`/`updateActiveAreas`/`stopHeldChannel`/delayed recovery/landing branch; engine tests).

### M98. Katana is absent from the playable 99LC catalog
- **Expected:** Katana is two-handed, refunds other cooldowns on skill hits, has execute/self-root finisher, interrupting overhead, aggressive multi-slash leap, bleed cashout/refresh, parry, invulnerable side steps, disarm and a true-damage lightning dash that resets on a kill.
- **Actual before the fix:** no Katana definition exists and the runtime lacks bleed lifecycle, true damage, cooldown-on-hit refunds, self-root, cast interruption and kill-reset hooks (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** land a Katana skill while another skill is cooling down → no such weapon or cooldown flow exists.
- **Fix direction:** add the complete two-hand kit on generic status, cooldown and action-lock primitives.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `twohand-katana` implements both five-input sets and refunds only other Katana cooldowns on skill hits. The kit includes low-health execute/self-root, unconditional cast-interrupt overhead, dodge-aware multi-hit invulnerable flight, Symbol-gated bleed cashout/doubling, timer refresh, reactable short-release parry, directional invulnerable hops, disarming iaido and armor-breaking true-damage flash that resets only its own cooldown on a kill. Dance/flight invulnerability covers the whole action and every authored critical outcome emits the common `CRIT` cue (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `twohand-katana`; `engine.ts` Katana/parry/critical/cooldown branches; status/engine tests).

### M99. The generic hybrid sword does not implement the authored rhythm/opening sword
- **Expected:** correctly timed Zornhaw taps step forward and continue stagger pressure, matching aim motion raises their damage, fast taps cause Sword-only fatigue, elite/boss stagger pressure eventually grants visible Unstoppable, and Oberhaw may morph the current strike before an optional held Unterhaw follow-up.
- **Actual before the fix:** `hybrid-sword` is a generic fixed combo with no timing bands, fatigue, enemy opening state or contextual double-tap result. The schema also forces invented actions into unspecified gesture slots (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `types.ts`; `engine.ts`).
- **Failure scenario:** tap too quickly, at the intended rhythm, or after an enemy opening appears → all inputs resolve the same cyclic attack.
- **Fix direction:** add explicit rhythm/opening state and allow disabled gesture slots instead of inventing undocumented moves.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16; reworked 2026-07-18 and 2026-07-21 — `hybrid-sword` keeps all moves on the left runtime hand and explicitly disables unspecified slots. Zornhaw bypasses legacy tap classification on press, alternates real side-to-side colliders, advances even on the fatigue-triggering strike and drives configurable body-centered rhythm/fatigue feedback; its former aim-motion damage scaling moved to Axe Tap. Configurable stagger may be disabled as a subsystem; otherwise elites/bosses gain five seconds of visible Unstoppable after six default half-second staggers. Oberhaw immediately morphs an active Zornhaw, may cancel fatigue caused by that morphed strike, resets timing, uses reduced base damage or the larger opening execution, and marks an executed victim so any later death resets its cooldown. Unterhaw is now exclusively a held continuation of an already-fired Oberhaw, with separate cooldown and target-priority rules detailed by M120 (`public/99lc/game-config.json` `hybrid-sword`; `engine.ts` `press`/`applySwordRhythm`/`morphSwordAttack`/`applySwordStagger`/`executePendingUnterhau`; Builder, status and engine tests).

### M100. 99LC lacks in-world charge/readiness feedback and collider-faithful traces
- **Expected:** the player has color-coded gesture readiness, segmented charge sectors for the actual release outcomes, becomes transparent during invulnerability, and every attack leaves a fading trace of its real collider.
- **Actual before the fix:** charge feedback is a sidebar bar clamped at the global hold threshold, invulnerability only changes the outline to white, and melee/dash/projectile effects are approximate lines that omit dead zones/radius/swept geometry (`WeaponCooldowns.vue`; `engine.ts` `renderPlayer`/`renderEffect`).
- **Failure scenario:** charge the spear through its release sectors or pole-vault through danger → the arena gives no exact release boundary and the body remains opaque.
- **Fix direction:** derive Canvas cues and fading traces from the same geometry/state used by collision, while mirroring them in the accessible HUD.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — five stable gesture colors drive both HUD rows and world-space readiness pips; charge actions render threshold-accurate segmented rings, armed sectors and recovery blocking. The player is translucent through the complete resolved invulnerability action, and authored sweet spots/critical skills share a world-space `CRIT` cue. Sectors, strict annuli, capsules, circles and rotating sweeps share one collision/outline resolver; projectiles, dashes and low-frame-rate rotations sample their swept volume and feed those exact samples into fading traces. Empty slots and disabled/recovering gestures no longer advertise usable inputs, and the Builder exposes strict dead-zone editing alongside the other collider controls (`colliders.ts`; `engine.ts` `dashStepColliders`/`updateActiveAreas`/`damageEnemy`/`renderColliderTrace`/`renderActionCues`/`renderPlayer`; `BuilderDrawer.vue`; `WeaponCooldowns.vue`; `TouchControls.vue`; collider/engine/page tests).

### M101. Existing schema-v2 browser overrides would strand testers on the obsolete nine weapons
- **Expected:** deploying the seven-weapon catalog upgrades the exact previous browser override while preserving unrelated player, enemy, room and balance tuning.
- **Actual before the fix:** `loadLastChancesConfig` returns localStorage before fetching `/99lc/game-config.json`; a saved schema-v2 catalog therefore permanently wins over the new server definition (`features/last-chances/config.ts` `loadLastChancesConfig`).
- **Failure scenario:** a tester with any saved Builder override opens the new build → the old spear, knife, bow, hammer, shield, orb and swords remain active.
- **Fix direction:** fetch the current schema-v3 definition, merge compatible non-weapon override tuning, adopt the new weapon/loadout/content references and persist the migrated result.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — loading a schema-v2 override now fetches the current server definition, preserves compatible player/input/enemy/progression/room tuning, adopts schema-v3 weapons/loadout/content references, validates the merged result and persists the one-time upgrade. Schema-v1's narrow geometry/tap repair remains separate, and schema-v3 overrides still load without a network merge (`config.ts` `mergeLegacyOverrideWithCurrent`/`loadLastChancesConfig`; exact migration/error tests).

### M102. Post-roll character replacement bypasses mutually exclusive roster pairs
- **Expected:** the current shared exclusions—LeCrisp/Толя/ScamRat pairwise, plus HardKitty/Эрен Йегер—can never coexist through a normal roster assignment, including guaranteed rewards, draft confirmation and the admin test picker (`CHARACTERS.md`; `StartGameLogic.AreMutuallyExclusiveCharacters`).
- **Actual before the fix:** natural rolling removed both counterparts, but the queued-loot replacement claimed only an exact-name bot or the first bot, while the admin test picker swapped only an exact duplicate (`WebGameService.cs:298-316,1194-1213`). Draft confirmation also rejected only exact duplicates. These post-roll paths could therefore introduce either forbidden pair after a valid bot roster had already been built.
- **Failure scenario:** create an admin test game as HardKitty after the preliminary bot roll assigns Эрен Йегер → Eren remains in the game; queued HardKitty or either member of the LeCrisp/Толя pair has the same failure mode.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `StartGameLogic.AreMutuallyExclusiveCharacters` is now the shared roster rule. Natural/draft pools and competing guaranteed reservations apply it; a queued reward or admin test choice replaces/swaps the incompatible bot seat; and both web and Discord draft confirmation revalidate the pair before replacing a bridge. An incompatible reservation that loses the shuffled reservation race remains queued for a later game rather than being consumed.
- **Rework follow-up:** 2026-07-27 — ScamRat joined the LeCrisp/Толя redirect-character group; the same shared predicate automatically extends natural, guaranteed, draft, simulation and admin-test validation to all three pairings.

## 99 Last Chances authored-weapon regression review (2026-07-16)

### M103. Tap-parries resolve after the enemy's reactable window has already ended
- **Expected:** releasing a short parry input during the visible 95–190 ms final windup can stop the strike, while a double/held gesture cannot receive a free overlapping parry.
- **Actual before the fix:** the gesture recognizer waited the full 260 ms double-tap window before emitting `tap`; every shipped parry window was shorter, so a reactive parry always arrived late. Arming on raw press had previously made every compound gesture parry for free (`features/last-chances/gestures.ts`; interim `engine.ts` `press`/`performAttack`; `public/99lc/game-config.json` enemy `parryWindowMs`).
- **Failure scenario:** press and release Spear guard when the enemy marker opens → the enemy attack resolves before `tap`; start a double tap instead → a raw-press implementation would overlap guard and shove.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — a short first release creates a provisional directional parry immediately. If it catches an attack/projectile, the queued compound gesture commits only the parry; otherwise the provisional collider is canceled when the double/held gesture resolves. Pause/blur commits a consumed provisional state and cancels an unused one (`engine.ts` `beginProvisionalTapParry`/`commitOrCancelProvisionalParry`/`consumeActiveParry`; focused engine tests).

### M104. Charged invulnerability can end before the charged action
- **Expected:** every action marked invulnerable keeps the player translucent and immune for its complete resolved movement.
- **Actual before the fix:** invulnerability used the unscaled `invulnerabilityMs`, while charge bands could lengthen `durationMs`. The late Axe leap lasted 984 ms but became vulnerable after 900 ms; Katana dance remained active for 760 ms but lost immunity after 720 ms (`engine.ts` interim `performAttack`; `public/99lc/game-config.json` Axe/Katana attacks).
- **Failure scenario:** take a hit during the final 84 ms of a late Axe flight → damage lands while the leap animation/collider is still active.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — any authored invulnerability is raised to at least the fully resolved attack duration, and the same authoritative timer drives damage immunity, snapshot state and body transparency (`engine.ts` `performAttack`/`damagePlayer`/`renderPlayer`; charged Axe/Katana regression).

### M105. Spear inner radii still hit enemies standing body-to-body
- **Expected:** Spear damage starts beyond the protected close zone even for large enemy bodies, not merely enemy centers.
- **Actual before the fix:** annular collision accepted any target circle touching the damaging band. At real contact distance `player radius + enemy radius`, a 16–44-unit enemy still overlapped the 52/56/62-unit inner edge (`colliders.ts` interim `annulusHitsCircle`/`sectorHitsCircle`; shipped Spear colliders).
- **Failure scenario:** stand touching a large enemy and use the basic sweep → its center is inside the dead zone, but its outer circle receives full damage.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — collider definitions may opt into `strictInnerRange`, which rejects a target whose body overlaps the protected circle. Every relevant Spear basic/thrust/ram/release/stance collider enables it, the dash collider carries the same exclusion, and Quick Tune exposes the flag (`types.ts`; `colliders.ts` `resolveAttackCollider`/`colliderHitsCircle`; `engine.ts` `dashStepColliders`; `BuilderDrawer.vue`; contact-radius tests).

### M106. The charged Spear kick does not place enemies in Spear critical range
- **Expected:** the late “Критовая дистанция” kick pushes the target into the critical band and gains distance from its stronger charge.
- **Actual before the fix:** both kick bands teleported the target to the same 80-unit fallback; the resolved knockback multiplier affected neither final position nor the next Spear sweet spot, which begins around 140 units (`engine.ts` interim Spear shove/kick branch; `public/99lc/game-config.json` `spearKick`).
- **Failure scenario:** release the late charged kick and immediately use the basic Spear sweep → the target remains well inside the critical band.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — `spearKick` derives final range from the larger of its base target and resolved charge-scaled knockback. The shipped late band lands at 167.4 units; the knob remains external as `targetDistancePerKnockback` (`engine.ts` Spear kick branch; `public/99lc/game-config.json`; focused engine test).

### M107. Late Spear throws neither pin at the room perimeter nor keep carried enemies outside walls
- **Expected:** a late throw pins carried enemies only when the projectile meets an obstacle or room boundary and leaves their circle on navigable floor.
- **Actual before the fix:** only obstacles counted as walls; range/time expiry silently cleared carried IDs. On obstacle collision, the enemy had already been copied to the projectile's post-step point, potentially inside or beyond the wall (`engine.ts` interim `updateProjectiles`/`pinProjectileTargets`).
- **Failure scenario:** throw through an obstacle-free line toward the arena edge → the target travels outside the room and receives no wall pin; a large enemy hitting an obstacle can be stunned inside it.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — swept projectile checks now include arena bounds. A collision clamps the projectile, backtracks each carried enemy opposite travel until its full radius is outside obstacles/bounds, then applies the authored pin; natural range/time expiry still releases without an air pin (`engine.ts` `updateProjectiles`/`pinProjectileTargets`; obstacle-free perimeter regression).

### M108. Knife-spider turns toward the player during its locked leap and hides its rear
- **Expected:** once the leap vector locks, both movement and facing stay on that vector through a miss so following the spider exposes its back.
- **Actual before the fix:** `updateEnemies` unconditionally faced every attacker toward the player's current position before advancing a locked leap. A dodged Knife-spider therefore looked backward at its pursuer when the capture window opened (`engine.ts` interim `updateEnemies`/`updateEnemyAttack`).
- **Failure scenario:** dodge, run behind the spider along its path and press interact → rear-dot validation treats the player as standing in front.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — an attacker with a locked leap direction retains its facing until the leap finishes; the missed-spider stun/capture window therefore uses the actual rear hemisphere (`engine.ts` `updateEnemies`/`finishEnemyAttack`/`capturableKnifeSpider`; simulated miss regression).

### M109. Charged Claw dash scratches every enemy along its traversal
- **Expected:** charge controls travel range; the damaging scratch happens once at the dash endpoint, while Chemical may independently leave a trail.
- **Actual before the fix:** `clawDash` was a normal damaging dash, so every swept traversal collider dealt the full scratch and there was no endpoint attack (`engine.ts` interim `performDash`/`updatePlayer`; `public/99lc/game-config.json` `clawDash`).
- **Failure scenario:** align one enemy halfway through the dash and one at the destination → both receive the same full scratch.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-16 — Claw travel is traversal-only and keeps its exact fading path/optional Chemical trail; completion spawns one externally tuned 82-unit, 110-degree endpoint scratch collider.
