- **Actual before the fix:** the final prologue card narrated the enemy's jump as already completed, then tier 1 randomly selected `false-apartment`, `combat-hall` or `chest-gallery` and rolled Нож-паук only at weight 2. A run could therefore begin in another room against other enemies and revisit the supposed opening apartment later. `LastChances.vue.newGeneration` also reopened every prologue page on each manual seed reset.
- **Failure scenario:** finish the opening cards and choose the only available route → generation may start in `combat-hall` with servants and no Нож-паук. Press “New generation” → the same five story pages block play again even though only the deterministic run layout was replaced.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — the shipped graph begins with an authored tier-0 `opening` node whose false apartment has a fixed single Нож-паук encounter; procedural I–VI retain their visible numbering and cannot reuse that room. The final prologue card stops before the leap resolves, and `newGeneration` replaces the run without replaying story pages. Migration detects authentic pre-opening bundled topology, inserts the canonical encounter while preserving custom tuning and now has a true v7-shape regression (`public/99lc/game-config.json`; `config.ts` `backfillAuthoredOpening`; `LastChances.vue` `newGeneration`/`runMapNodes`; `RunMapOverlay.vue`).

### M146. 99LC mouse gesture hands are not independent while a button is already held

- **Expected:** primary and secondary inputs are two independent five-gesture channels, so either mouse button can be pressed or released while the other remains held (`99LC - Описание механик`, document `1E2fiLlO1GRSoKMKySWElbgD1m9UuyqgUIp3Un0QCjVc`; prototype requirements document `1j9mTfhZJM2pxANJa9GQllPkxfgOywadYfYHykz8U8Mg`).
- **Actual before the fix:** the canvas drove both channels only from `pointerdown`/`pointerup`. For a mouse, Pointer Events emit `pointerdown` only when the pointer changes from no buttons to at least one and `pointerup` only when it changes back to none; an additional LMB/RMB transition while the other button remained held was therefore invisible. `pointercancel` also released only `event.button`, which can strand the other recognizer (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` pre-fix `attachEvents`/`handlePointerDown`/`handlePointerUp`).
- **Failure scenario:** hold LMB, press RMB, release LMB while continuing to hold RMB → the right-hand gesture never starts and the left-hand release may not settle until the final mouse button is released. A canceled captured pointer can likewise leave one hand permanently pressed.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — mouse Pointer Events now reconcile both hands from `event.buttons` on movement as well as down/up edges. `pointercancel` and `lostpointercapture` cancel both recognizers without resolving attacks, suppress the stale stream until every button is released, and blur/scheme replacement reseed the stream. Regressions cover both chord orders, both partial releases, lost capture, blur/hot-switch recovery and the empty-right-hand Ninja RMB chord (`engine.ts` `syncPointerButtons`/`cancelPointerButtons`; `gestures.ts` `cancel`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M147. Hidden TheBoys combination cards executed before their combination was learned

- **Expected:** a secret combination is mechanically inert and unnamed until the exact first-four upgrade distribution reveals it. «Убийственная Парочка» should drain Justice only after Francie x2 + Kimiko x2 resolves as the active combination.
- **Actual before the fix:** `ApplyKillingCoupleJustice` selected TheBoys by finding the «Убийственная Парочка» card in `GameCharacter.Passive`. That hidden `Visible=false` card exists from game initialization, so every TheBoys could drain a dangerous enemy's Justice from the first fight and receive a personal log naming the still-locked combination.
- **Failure scenario:** enter round 1 without any upgrades and resolve a fight against a TooGood/TooStronk enemy with Justice → their Justice is transferred and the TheBoys log reveals «Убийственная Парочка».
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — `TheBoys.HasActiveCombination` now requires the real TheBoys holder, the exact `ActiveCombination` value and that combination's revealed card. `ApplyKillingCoupleJustice` uses this lifecycle gate for both fight sides, so a locked card cannot execute or reveal its name (v5.1.9).

### M148. Смертельный вирус silently skipped installed marks after either side died

- **Expected:** infection installs a delayed end-game liability. Every infected mark settles for its recorded source; later death of the infected seat or Francie does not cure the mark.
- **Actual before the fix:** `HandleLastRound` filtered out every dead infected player and then rejected every mark whose source TheBoys was dead. A virus that visibly infected and spread could therefore remove no points at all, despite retaining its state and owner-visible infected-name list.
- **Failure scenario:** infect two players, then let one infected player or TheBoys die before final settlement → that mark, or every mark respectively, is omitted from the debit/credit loop.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — settlement now iterates every installed mark and resolves Naruto score successors before applying the debit. unknown_bug immunity, СуперМудень cancellation and the intended zero-floor/full-nominal-credit rule remain unchanged; the simultaneous balance change sets each debit/credit to 3 (`CheckIfReady.HandleLastRound`; `TheBoys.VirusPointsPerInfected`; v5.1.9).

### M149. 99LC move unlocks survive death despite the no-progress retry rule

- **Expected:** death restarts the same deterministic generation from its beginning with no completed-room or upgrade progress retained; only the authored stat erosion, Chance loss and specifically sourced run-persistent exceptions remain (project GDD `1mpT03MyXtV1tW-j2uFbG7KvdnYqJKnBOc2GyTBw5hcE`).
- **Actual before the fix:** `resetAttempt` deliberately preserved every move-quest completion, pending unlock and unlocked gesture, resetting only the current room's kill counters. A player could therefore permanently unlock attacks by dying, despite the main retry invariant (`features/last-chances/engine.ts` pre-fix `resetAttempt`; pre-fix regression “keeps earned unlocks across a death”).
- **Failure scenario:** unlock double tap, die before completing the generation, then retry → the plan and enemies reset but double tap remains available, carrying attempt progression through death.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — ordinary `resetAttempt` now recreates both hand quest states, so unlocked moves, pending rewards, room kills and elite checklists restart with the deterministic run. An accepted boss altar explicitly snapshots and restores the exact quest state for its one-use direct-room retry; without that narrow source-specific exception, a boss-only checkpoint could remove moves whose kill quests cannot be earned there. Both paths have regressions (`engine.ts` `createHandMoveQuestState`/`resetAttempt`/`resolveAltar`/`retryAttempt`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M150. 99LC attack commits preserve residual movement and core combat thresholds bypass the definition

- **Expected:** an attack button immediately stops the character and begins the attack (Discord mechanics message `1152223681192402954`). The prototype/GDD also requires gameplay values to remain externally editable.
- **Actual before the fix:** committing an ordinary attack left acceleration velocity intact until an authored root or later recovery happened. Move-quest targets, action-direction dead zone, minimum damage, parry/reveal timing, Spear release/kick/breakthrough behavior, Sword opening damage, Chemical trails, Katana dodge cadence, spin assistance, Claw parity bleed, reflected/enemy/turret projectile behavior and Cockroach Mother hole timing were fixed literals in `engine.ts`.
- **Failure scenario:** begin moving, commit an unrooted attack and the character slides through its opening frames. Changing the affected balance behavior then requires recompiling TypeScript instead of editing `game-config.json` or the Builder.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — schema v9 adds validated `combat`, `input.actionDirectionDeadZone` and `progression.moveQuestKillsRequired` controls. A valid attack press and its committed resolution both clear residual walking velocity when `combat.attackStopsMovement` is enabled. The full audited gameplay-literal inventory now reads the external definition: minimum parry/damage and reveal durations; move-quest target; Spear, Sword, Chemical trail, Katana, spin-assist and Claw-parity thresholds; reflection range/pierce; hostile-projectile knockback; turret alarm and muzzle geometry; and Mother entrance/recovery/hole-selection values. Projectile attack speeds must be positive, incoming projectile knockback moves the player through collision-safe room movement, and no misleading enemy “pierce” setting is exposed for a projectile whose only hostile target is the single player. Common controls appear in both Builder languages, behavior-specific values remain editable through Builder JSON, v7/v8 overrides receive compatibility defaults, and override regressions prove every group reaches runtime (`types.ts` `LastChancesCombatDefinition`/enemy/turret/Mother definitions; `config.ts` `backfillRunTuningFields`; `plan.ts` `makeNode`; `engine.ts` `press`/`performAttack`/`updateProjectiles`/`updateTurrets`/`enemyCombatProfile`/`updateCockroachMotherRetreat`; `BuilderDrawer.vue`; `public/99lc/game-config.json`).

### M151. 99LC omits the paid same-tier detour from the authored run graph

- **Expected:** after clearing a room, the run-layout diagram attached to Discord level-design message `1158329506973626468` lets the player sacrifice half of current physical and mental health to enter a neighboring room of the tier just traversed instead of moving only upward.
- **Actual before the fix:** `finishRoomTransition()` exposed only `currentNode.nextNodeIds`; `chooseNode()` had no paid-route concept, the snapshot did not distinguish such routes, and the map could neither explain nor render the sacrifice.
- **Failure scenario:** clear any tier with an unvisited horizontal neighbor → the map offers only next-tier rooms, removing an authored risk/reward route for extra combat and loot.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — after a room resolves, its immediate unvisited horizontal neighbors join the ordinary upward choices in stable order. `sacrificeNodeIds` marks them in the snapshot and run map; selecting one multiplies current HP and mental health by validated `progression.sameTierSacrificeRatio` (0.5 shipped), records the route in the attempt and prevents repeat visits. The overlay labels the cost and draws visited/available horizontal edges without changing the immutable generated graph (`engine.ts` `sameTierSacrificeNeighbors`/`finishRoomTransition`/`chooseNode`; `LastChances.vue` `runMapEdges`; `RunMapOverlay.vue`; `engine.spec.ts`).

### M152. 99LC DualSense documentation published stale release hysteresis

- **Expected:** `docs/BALANCE-CONSTANTS.md` is the source of truth for the shipped controller thresholds and must agree with `input.dualsense`.
- **Actual before the fix:** the balance row said release 0.14 and hysteresis 0.08, while the first shipped DualSense pass had already used release 0.16 and hysteresis 0.06. Following the row would therefore recreate a different neutral/re-arm boundary than production.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — the row now records release 0.16/hysteresis 0.06 and the pass-2 activation/gate grid 0.25/0.25/0.50/0.75/0.95, 450 ms arm dwell and 900 ms telegraph repeat. The adjacent profile rows now match the shipped ramp/gate/tension tuples, and the new depth-ruler, armed-feedback and Axe-max rows keep the complete pass-2 tuning externally auditable (`docs/BALANCE-CONSTANTS.md`; `public/99lc/game-config.json` `input.dualsense`; `features/last-chances/config.ts`).

### M153. 99LC arena was flattened despite the required isometric presentation

- **Expected (designer instruction, 2026-07-26):** restore the original diamond-shaped isometric arena and elevated obstacle faces; the seeded run map and its paid same-tier routes remain unchanged.
- **Actual before the fix:** the Canvas projected each authored room as an axis-aligned top-down rectangle with one uniform screen scale. Obstacles were flat rectangles whose `elevation` changed only their shadow, and entity anchors had been shifted for that presentation (`engine.ts` pre-fix `ArenaLayout`/`worldToScreen`/`renderObstacle`/entity renderers; `engine.spec.ts` pre-fix projection regression).
- **Failure scenario:** enter any combat room → the floor fills a rectangular 16:9 frame, walls have no raised top/side geometry and movement axes are not shown on the intended isometric diamond.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — `worldToScreen`/`screenToWorld` again use the inverse diamond transform, the empty and active floors occupy the same fitted isometric diamond, depth-sorted bodies use their ground anchors, and `elevation` extrudes obstacle top/side faces. Ground circles, shockwaves and burst effects are sampled through the projection so their visual footprint still matches world-space combat geometry. Run generation, room coordinates, collision, movement, the authored opening and same-tier detours are unchanged (`engine.ts` `IsometricLayout`/`layout`/`worldToScreen`/`screenToWorld`/`renderObstacle`/`traceProjectedCircle`; `engine.spec.ts` isometric round-trip regression).

### M154. Вилтрумайтское вторжение could overwrite Космический ужас on the same round

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** **Космический ужас** has unconditional priority over every other match-ending mechanic.
- **Actual before the fix:** `Cthulhu.HandleEndOfRound` fired the horror and set `game.IsFinished`, but `CompleteRoundAsync` still called `OmniMan.TryTriggerInvasion` later in the same round. A due invasion ignored both `HorrorFired` and `IsFinished`, forced Omni-man to place 1 and made `CheckIfReady.HandleLastRound` select the invasion-only winner path.
- **Failure scenario:** the second idle Нечто round and Omni-man's delayed invasion mature together → the abyss serial fires first, but the later invasion serial/placement replaces the horror result.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — invasion evaluation and triggering now cancel their pending round whenever the horror has fired or another authoritative ending already owns the game. The final invasion-winner lookup also returns null under `HorrorFired`, so even stale dual state cannot outrank **Космический ужас** (`OmniMan.EvaluateInvasion`/`TryTriggerInvasion`/`GetInvasionWinner`; `Cthulhu.HandleEndOfRound`).

### M155. Стражи Земли ranked gross positive entries instead of the turn's final delta

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** the candidate comparison uses each enemy's net projected score delta for the turn; losses offset gains.
- **Actual before the fix:** `ApplyGuardiansOfTheGlobe` discarded every negative `ScoreEntry` before summing, so `+10/-20` ranked as `+10` instead of `-10` and could sleep ahead of a genuine `+5` enemy.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — the projection now sums all bonus entries at ×1 and all positive or negative regular entries at the recipient's current round multiplier. The mechanic still runs before **Башня Vought**, requires one strictly positive unique maximum and does nothing on ties or non-positive maxima (`OmniMan.ApplyGuardiansOfTheGlobe`).

### M156. Живое Оружие removed Homelander's passive cards but left his direct mechanics active

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** after Живое Оружие clears Homelander's passives, every part of his kit is inert for the rest of the match, just like the explicitly guarded name-based helpers of other characters.
- **Actual before the fix:** `ApplyRighteousness` and `ApplyVoughtTower` found Homelander by character name and continued to grant Moral/rage, arm the laser and transform score after the passive list had been cleared. The web mapper likewise kept projecting the private rage display. A future conditional score tied to Праведность would have had the same hole.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — Homelander's direct helpers now require their exact surviving passive. `DisablePassivesBeforeFights` calls `Homelander.OnPassivesDisabled`, which freezes the Seven score and clears the current laser target; web rage/revealer state is projected only while its source passive exists. Ordinary character state is preserved, but no removed passive continues to execute (`Homelander.HasPassive`/`OnPassivesDisabled`/`ApplyRighteousness`/`ApplyVoughtTower`; `TheBoys.DisablePassivesBeforeFights`; `GameStateMapper.MapPlayer`).

### M157. Protected Homelander point thefts could mint the thief's credit without debiting the victim

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** **Супер "Человек"** may reject the debit, but a protected transfer must then award no paired points to the thief or deferred reclaimer. D12's nominal-credit cushion remains intended only for an ordinary victim whose applied debit reaches the zero floor.
- **Actual before the fix:** several transfer-shaped mechanics issued the Homelander debit and source credit as independent mutations. The central receiver guard rejected only the debit, so Морок, Выгодная сделка, Роум, Большой куш, Кошачья засада, Глаза Итачи, Чернильная завеса, Сайтама's reclaim or Переписать историю could still create points or a later reclaim ledger from a protected victim.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — `Homelander.CanTransferFrom` is checked before each protected point-transfer pair and logs the existing sourced protection response. Итачи checks only after a positive steal amount exists; Octopus removes rejected liabilities from its own restore credit; Salldorum pays a replacement point only when an eligible recorded recipient was actually debited (or the original win legitimately had no positive recipient). Non-point side effects remain intentionally unchanged, as do TheBoys bypasses, the direct victory-point redirects from Еврей and Sharing is CARRYING!, Толя's multiplier marker and D12 for ordinary victims (`Homelander.CanTransferFrom`; `Cthulhu.HandleEndOfRound`; `Salldorum.RewriteHistory`; CP transfer call sites).

### M158. Космический ужас marked the game finished but allowed the rest of the round/final modifiers to run

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** **Космический ужас** ends the game immediately when its condition is detected and has priority over every remaining mechanic.
- **Actual before the fix:** `Cthulhu.HandleEndOfRound` set `IsFinished`, but `DoomsdayMachine.CalculateAllFights` continued through achievement tracking, every ordinary end-of-round passive, Guardians/Vought/invasion evaluation, Halflife settlement and the complete next-round tail. `HandleLastRound` then also ran predictions, virus, Цукуеми, AWDKA, Premade, Ziggurat, Jon, Sakura and winner flavor before moving the Вестник first.
- **Failure scenario:** the second idle Нечто round fires while another score/end-game passive is due → the abyss begins, but later mechanics still change the supposedly interrupted game's table and can delay finalization.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — the horror boundary now settles only score already earned by resolved fights/Нечто, alive-first orders the current table and returns directly from `CalculateAllFights`. `CheckIfReady` enters final settlement in the same tick; its horror branch skips every ordinary final modifier and only applies the living-Вестник placement rule. If the Вестник is dead or absent, the deserved first living player remains winner (`DoomsdayMachine.SettleCosmicHorrorRound`/`CalculateAllFights`; `CheckIfReady.HandleLastRound`; `Cthulhu.ApplyHeraldFinalPlacement`).

### M159. Round-8 Мадара could attack Нечто before the ordinary waiting guard

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** while Мадара waits for challengers on round 8, he cannot attack Нечто.
- **Actual before the fix:** `GameReactions.HandleAttack` recognized the Нечто sentinel and returned through `SubmitNechtoAttack` before reaching the later round-8 Madara reject. The event submitter itself checked only death and seal state, so Discord/web/bot callers could queue the isolated fight.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — `SubmitNechtoAttack` now rejects a round-8 Мадара authoritatively, and the Discord attack menu omits the sentinel for that actor. The shared web/place and bot paths reach the same guard (`Cthulhu.SubmitNechtoAttack`; `GameUpdateMess.GetAttackMenu`; `GameReactions.HandleAttack`).

### M160. An all-four-adept forced roster could duplicate an occupied adept

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-26):** if all four potential adepts are occupied, Ктулху does not create a duplicate. The four occupied adepts receive `Откликнуться на зов глубин`.
- **Actual before the fix:** `AvailableAdepts` fell back from an empty free list to all four templates. The pre-game coordinator also opened Ктулху's adept stage before `Зов глубин` and `TryBeginDepthsCallStage` explicitly rejected any roster whose original lineup contained Ктулху.
- **Failure scenario:** force Ктулху + mylorik + Братишка + Осьминожка + Краборак → Ктулху could replace itself with a second copy of an occupied adept, and the authored all-four prompt never opened.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — an empty availability list remains empty, ordinary Ктулху choice moved to its round-1 owner action, and the blocking all-four check runs independently of original Ктулху presence. The four occupied adepts receive the prompt and no duplicate bridge can be created (`Cthulhu.AvailableAdepts`/`RequiresPreGameStage`/`TryBeginDepthsCallStage`; `CheckIfReady.TickAsync`).

### M161. Bot Нечто attacks were also converted into Block

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected:** attacking Нечто consumes the turn as the bot's complete action; it cannot also receive a defensive Block.
- **Actual before the fix:** the event sentinel stored the attack in `PendingNechtoAttackers` rather than `WhoToAttackThisTurn`. The final missing-action fallback only inspected the ordinary target list/Block/Skip flags, logged a critical no-action error and assigned Block even though the Нечто attack was already queued.
- **Failure scenario:** activate Нечто with an all-bot all-four-adept roster → every bot eagerly submits place 7 → the fallback grants every attacker Block before resolving the queued event fights.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — the missing-action fallback excludes every player present in `PendingNechtoAttackers`, preserving the event attack as the sole action (`CheckIfReady.TickAsync`; `Cthulhu.SubmitNechtoAttack`).

### M162. Darksci's «Нахуй эту игру» never reached the round it describes

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected:** after round 8, whenever Дизмораль's zero Psyche makes Darksci unable to act, the public «Нахуй эту игру» must be in the log block players read *while choosing actions for that round* — that is the whole point of the line: it lets everyone (and the fair L2/L3 bots that parse it, `BotsBehavior.cs:993-1000`) stop wasting an attack on a skipping target.
- **Actual before the fix:** the marker was emitted at most once per game, guarded by the cumulative `GetAllGlobalLogs()`, from two points that both miss the round they describe. The round-9 write lives in the level-up tail (`GameReactions.cs`), but bots and auto-move players spend the mandatory point inside the readiness pipeline at the very end of the round (`CheckIfReady.cs:1455` → `HandleBotBehavior` → `HandleLvlUpBot`), after every human has already submitted — and `CalculateAllFights` replaces the per-round `GlobalLogs` immediately afterwards (`DoomsdayMachine.cs:290`). That unreadable write then satisfied the per-game guard and permanently silenced the round-10 announcement, exactly where he is still frozen and points are ×4. A holder who was already at 0 Psyche entering round 9 was never announced at all, because the transition-time zero check excludes rounds 3/5/7/9.
- **Failure scenario:** bot Darksci enters round 9 with 4 Psyche → level-up +1, Дизмораль −5 → 0 → forced Skip. The public line is written after everyone has acted and is wiped a moment later; round 10 stays silent, so players and fair bots attack a target that cannot fight back for the two decisive rounds.
- **Measured:** instrumented 40-game `Darksci`-fixed matchups, comparing the public block at the start of each round against the freeze actually resolved that round. Before: 4/34 frozen rounds carried the warning before actions (round 9: 0/15). After: **36/36**, with no false alarms (marker present in a round he was not frozen).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — the round-9 transition now announces the freeze as soon as it is arithmetically unavoidable (`Psyche + max level-up gain − 5 ≤ 0`, the pending point being worth at most +1 Psyche), in the same place as «Всё, у меня горит!» (`CP:6931-6950`); and both markers switched from the per-game `GetAllGlobalLogs()` guard to the per-round `GetGlobalLogs()` one, so each round he opens frozen re-announces it and round 9 no longer silences round 10 (`CP:6979-6994`; `GameReactions.cs:1651-1677`). Freeze mechanics are untouched: the −5 still fires on the mandatory level-up and rounds 3/5/7/9 still defer the zero check to that path (D1).

### M163. 99LC screen movement speed depended on the direction walked

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-26):** «движение должно быть равномерно быстрым» — holding any single movement key must carry the character across the screen at the same rate.
- **Actual before the fix:** `worldToScreen` normalized each world axis against *its own* room dimension (`x / arena.width`, `y / arena.height`) before mapping onto a diamond of fixed screen proportions. Every authored room is 16:9, so one world unit along Y covered `width / height ≈ 1.78×` more screen distance than one along X. Movement input is applied in world coordinates and the view is isometric, so W/S read on screen as up-right / down-left (fast) and A/D as up-left / down-right (slow); the pairing was symmetric under 180° because the transform is linear. `entityScale` already used a single isotropic scale for radii, so positions and sizes disagreed about the size of a world unit (`engine.ts` pre-fix `worldToScreen`/`screenToWorld`/`entityScale`; `engine.spec.ts` pre-fix projection regression).
- **Failure scenario:** enter any room and hold W, then D, across open floor → the character visibly crosses far more of the screen per second in the up-right direction than in the down-right one, and the floor grid's cells are squashed along one axis.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — a new `projection` helper is the single authority for each room's world→screen basis and gives both world axes the same scale; `worldToScreen`, `screenToWorld` and `entityScale` all derive from it. All four movement keys now cover identical screen distance. A 16:9 room is consequently drawn to scale as an elongated rhombus instead of a symmetric diamond, still spanning exactly the fitted `diamondWidth` × `diamondHeight` box and still horizontally centred, so M153's isometric presentation, the fitted layout, depth sorting, collision, world-space combat geometry and the seeded run plan are unchanged. The remaining ~1.55× difference between the horizontal and vertical *screen* directions is the projection's intended isometric foreshortening and was deliberately kept (`engine.ts` `IsometricProjection`/`projection`/`worldToScreen`/`screenToWorld`/`entityScale`; engine regressions for equal per-axis scale and the invertible rhombus).

### M164. 99LC characters froze when walking straight into a wall

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-26):** walking straight at a wall or any other obstacle must not stop the character on the spot; it should slide along that surface toward the wall's nearest end.
- **Actual before the fix:** `moveCircle` resolved a blocked step by trying only an X or a Y slide. A single-key step is axis-aligned and obstacle faces are axis-aligned, so a head-on step produced no non-zero tangent and fell through to `applyCornerCorrection`, which nudges at most 0.8 radius sideways and finds nothing free in the middle of a long wall. Stopping dead centre was M131's explicit carve-out and was asserted by its regression (`engine.ts` pre-fix `moveCircle`/`applyCornerCorrection`; `engine.spec.ts` pre-fix “stops dead centre against a long wall”).
- **Failure scenario:** hold a single movement key into the flat middle of an authored obstacle face or into the arena boundary → the character stops until the player manually steers away, instead of rounding the obstacle.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — `moveCircle` takes an opt-in `wallSlide`, used only by the ordinary walking call in `updatePlayer`. When no axis tangent exists, `slideAlongBlockingWall` identifies the blocking obstacle (or the arena boundary, whose ends are its corners), inflates its tangential extent by the body radius and spends the entire unresolved step distance sliding toward the nearer end at full speed; the chosen direction is remembered while contact persists and cleared on the first unblocked step, so abutting obstacles cannot make it oscillate. Diagonal contact still follows the player's own tangential input, and dashes, knockback and enemy movement deliberately do not opt in, so forced movement still lands against the wall it hit rather than drifting off it — this supersedes M131's dead-centre carve-out for walking only (`engine.ts` `moveCircle`/`slideAlongBlockingWall`/`wallSlideMemo`/`updatePlayer`; engine regressions for the obstacle face, the arena boundary and the unchanged forced-movement path).

### M165. Предположения exposed every public character before the account unlocked them

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-27):** manual character assumptions must follow the Store unlock boundary: a player may choose only public characters already present in that account's `SeenCharacters`.
- **Actual before the fix:** `GameStateMapper` sent its process-wide public characters.json catalog to every live player, `GameReactions.HandlePredic1` built Discord's `predict-2` menu from every visible character, and the web/Discord write handlers validated only public visibility. A new account could therefore browse and submit characters it had never played, while Store correctly hid the same locked entries (`GameStateMapper.ToDto`; `GameReactions.HandlePredic1`/`HandlePredic2`; `WebGameService.Predict`/`DeathNoteWrite`; Store reference `WebGameService.BuildStoreState`).
- **Failure scenario:** an account whose `SeenCharacters` contains only its first rolled character opens any opponent's prediction picker → the complete public roster is searchable and a forged request for any one of those locked names is accepted.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-27 — personalized live DTOs intersect the public assumption catalog with a lock-protected snapshot of the requester's `SeenCharacters`; both initial reads and SignalR broadcasts supply the account. Discord builds `predict-2` from the same intersection. Web prediction/Death Note writes and Discord `predict-2` writes independently reject locked names, closing forged requests; passive-earned auto-fills remain outside this manual-selection gate. Spectator and persisted replay catalogs remain the non-interactive public base catalog (`GameStateMapper.ToDto`; `WebGameService.GetGameState`/`Predict`/`DeathNoteWrite`; `GameNotificationService`; `GameReactions.HandlePredic1`/`HandlePredic2`).

### M166. Eren's forced-last crossing ejected Джон Сноу from an active Черный Замок

- **Severity:** Major
- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-27):** the opening Черный Замок hold keeps Jon at exact place 4 through action rounds 1–3 unless an effect actually moves Jon; Eren's Овца в загоне moves Eren himself to place 6 and must not count as such an effect.
- **Actual before the fix:** score sorting first restored active Castle Black by moving Jon to list index 3, but the following `ErenYeager.MoveToLast` removed an Eren ranked above him and appended Eren at the end. That list removal shifted Jon to index 2/place 3, and `JonSnow.FinalizePositionEffects` then interpreted the incidental shift as a direct move and cancelled the hold (`Naruto.OrderLeaderboard`; `DoomsdayMachine.CompleteRoundAsync`; `ErenYeager.MoveToLast`; `JonSnow.FinalizePositionEffects`).
- **Failure scenario:** Jon opens in Castle Black and Eren earns enough round-1 score to sort above place 4 → the opening round-2 leaderboard pins Eren to place 6 by crossing the castle cell, shifts Jon to place 3 and releases him two action rounds early despite no Drop or Jon-targeting mover.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-27 — `ErenYeager.MoveToLast` snapshots whether Jon is actively holding the exact castle cell before moving Eren and restores that cell afterward. Eren remains at place 6, while a prior direct mover that already displaced Jon leaves the snapshot false and still cancels the hold through the existing finalizer (`ErenYeager.MoveToLast`; `JonSnow.IsHoldingBlackCastle`/`RestoreBlackCastlePosition`/`FinalizePositionEffects`).

### M167. Repeated quality breaks inverted Skill and Moral instead of stopping at zero

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** each Int-pool break removes 10% Skill contribution and each Psyche-pool break removes 20% effective Moral, but neither penalty may invert the sign. Earned Skill must never become combat debt and positive Moral must never become negative Moral.
- **Actual before the fix:** `GetIntelligenceQualitySkillBonus` and `GetPsycheQualityMoralBonus` returned their unbounded linear multipliers. The eleventh Int break produced a negative Skill multiplier; the sixth Psyche break produced a negative Moral multiplier.
- **Fixed:** both quality getters now floor the computed multiplier at `0m`. Counters remain unbounded, so further breaks still create debt that future positive quality recovery must repay, but the exposed contribution cannot cross below zero (`CharacterClass.GetIntelligenceQualitySkillBonus`/`GetPsycheQualityMoralBonus`).

### M168. Parallel fights shared Justice/live class and could activate Король Сервера mid-batch

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** every fight in one round is simultaneous. It may apply legitimate self-contained ForOneFight changes such as Геральт's current-fight class, but no result or persistent mutation from one fight may change another fight's input stats, class, Justice or bonus treatment.
- **Actual before the fix:** `FightCharacter` was reused across the round and `CharacterClass.DeepCopy` shared its mutable `Justice` object with `GameCharacter`. Kimiko and **Убийственная Парочка** therefore changed Justice seen by later fights. Several Мишень/nemesis/class branches mixed live `GameCharacter` with fight values. A Skill gain could call `JonSnow.TryBecomeKing` immediately, letting **Король Сервера** double a bonus from a later fight in the same calculation.
- **Fixed:** after approved pre-loop preparation, every player's combat state is frozen as immutable `RoundFightCharacter`; every actual pair receives fresh deep clones. `DeepCopy` now detaches Justice while retaining shared `Status`. Combat class/Мишень/nemesis/Justice reads use the current `FightCharacter`, including legitimate ForOneFight overrides. Kimiko and **Убийственная Парочка** alter clone Justice for current math and settle the lasting transfer separately on `GameCharacter`. Jon's 228 transition is held by `IsFightBatchActive` through all main and Нечто combat results, then committed before ordinary end-of-round. Нечто also clones the frozen template when available (`DoomsdayMachine.DeepCopyGameCharacterToFightCharacter`/`FreezeRoundFightCharacters`/`PrepareIndependentFightCharacters`/`CalculateAllFights`; `CharacterClass.DeepCopy`; `TheBoys.ApplyKillingCoupleJustice`; `Cthulhu.ResolveNechtoAttacks`; `JonSnow.TryBecomeKing`).

### M169. A 1–1 stat split with the third stat tied awarded neither side the core RPS bonus

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** when each player wins one of Int/Str/Speed and the third comparison ties, the categories they won fight by the fundamental relation Int > Speed > Str > Int. The player whose won stat counters the opponent's won stat receives the ordinary +5 Versatility weighing.
- **Actual before the fix:** `CalculateStep1` awarded ±5 only when the raw count of won categories differed. A 1–1 split always added zero, despite the player-facing design promising +5 to the won-stat anti-class winner.
- **Fixed:** the versatility block detects `wins == losses == 1`, resolves the two won categories through `CharacterClass.NemesisOf`, and applies the same ±5/log/DTO delta as an ordinary majority (`CalculateRounds.CalculateStep1`).

### M170. Тигр's paired loss survived a successful 2→3 «3-0 обоссан» trigger

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** with a pre-round 2–0 series against one enemy, one simultaneous loss and one win against that enemy must preserve the pair long enough for the win to reach 3–0 and trigger the passive; the paired loss is then consumed.
- **Actual before the fix:** the first pass correctly skipped resetting a series already at 2 when the same target appeared in both outcome lists, but left that loss in `WhoToLostThisRound`. The win then triggered 3–0, after which the final leftover-loss cleanup reset the just-triggered series anyway.
- **Fixed:** the ≥2 paired branch removes every matching loss before continuing. The win advances 2→3, pays +3 regular/+30 Skill/+3 Moral and the victim penalties once, and no stale loss remains for final cleanup (`CharacterPassives` end-of-round `3-0 обоссан` case).

### M171. OPEN — result/state aggregators still depend on sequential fight order

- **Core boundary now fixed:** M168 makes stats, class and Justice inputs order-independent. This finding covers persistent **outputs**, limited resources and queue rewrites whose intended same-batch aggregation cannot be inferred safely.
- **Order-sensitive counters/series:** mylorik **Спарта** `LostTimes`; HardKitty **Доебаться** (including defense-clear on any incoming resolved fight); Weed/`WinStreak`; achievement `ConsecutiveWins`.
- **Order-sensitive marks/state:** Homelander Rage→later laser; Eren hatred marks and bounded **Овца в загоне** +1-loss/−2-win final stat; TheBoys virus same-batch propagation; Toxic Mate **Get cancer** transfer/`BlockMoralGain`; Итачи Crows/Цукуеми; Goblin population ceil/growth.
- **Live economy/consequences:** unknown_bug Exploit observation vs commit; Darksci **Повезло** copying live score; Omni-man **Подземный Поезд** stealing live score/Moral; Harm/resist/Drop settlement and attribution.
- **Later-batch mutation:** Rick Portal rewrites remaining queues; Kratos deaths cancel later submitted fights.
- **Limited-resource priority requiring an explicit designer choice:** Изанаги uses, BFG charge and causal waves, every-third **Монтировка**, next Portal charge, next-fight Salldorum capsule.
- **Decision needed:** for each family choose sum, maximum, last event, named priority, or one use per complete batch; also state whether a collision consumes a charge. Do not preserve the current loop order accidentally. ScamRat's Justice cap and headcrab maturation were checked and are not remaining combat-input leaks under the frozen template.

### M172. Custom Psyche logs silently bypassed the shared immunity funnel

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** “does this loss write the ordinary «игрок психанул» line?” and “does the receiver have a Psyche-loss immunity?” are independent decisions. Every real loss checks the same five guards; the standard global line is on by default and only an explicit custom-text effect turns it off.
- **Actual before the fix:** `MinusPsycheLog` bundled the five guards with an unconditional default log. Дизмораль needed its own authored public records and therefore called raw `AddPsyche(-5)`, accidentally making its loss bypass Спокойствие and M.M. calmness. Every future custom-log passive faced the same invisible trap.
- **Fixed:** the bridge owns one `MinusPsyche(game, negativeDelta, source, suppressDefaultGlobalLog:false)` funnel and always targets its own persistent `GameCharacter`. It returns whether the loss landed, checks unknown_bug, protected Homelander, reanimated Madara, Спокойствие and M.M. calmness in one place, and writes «игрок психанул» unless only that line is explicitly suppressed. All ordinary callers use the default; Дизмораль uses suppression without bypassing immunity. Raw negative mutators remain only for audited self-costs, temporary-stat rollback and structural transformations such as Морок (`GamePlayerBridgeClass.MinusPsyche`; `GameReactions` Дизмораль tail; `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md`; conflit_mech 5.4).

### M173. Joining a live bot seat inherited the bot's false predictions and private AI memory

- **Expected:** a human taking ownership of a bot seat inherits the seat's public/gameplay state, not guesses or inference that the human never submitted. A reconnect to an already-human seat must remain unchanged.
- **Actual before the fix:** `JoinWebGame` only replaced identity/account fields on the existing bridge. Its `Predict`, `AiKnowledge`, persistent playstyle, difficulty probe and consecutive-Block counter survived, so the new player immediately saw—and could ultimately score—the bot's often-wrong assumptions.
- **Fixed:** the shared bot→human transfer helper replaces the prediction list and clears every AI-owned field before the bridge publishes its human identity, so the lock-free state mapper can observe neither a stale sheet nor an in-place collection mutation. Ordinary join always transfers a strict bot; curated-lobby seating resets only on a real ownership change. Round 8 receives a blank unconfirmed sheet only for a character allowed to predict, while earlier/later rounds retain the ordinary confirmation state. Existing-player reconnects preserve real human predictions (`WebGameService.ResetBotOwnedStateForHuman`/`JoinWebGame`/`SeatAdminLobbyHuman`).

### M174. Halflife 3 postponements replaced Gordon's ordinary score multiplier with ×1

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** `P^P` calculates profit from an actual HL3 release; it must not replace the ordinary score pipeline merely because an attempt was postponed. Gordon's regular `P` always settles at the real ×1/×2/×4 (or Толя ×1), then release adds a flat profit or postponement subtracts a flat 1/2/3.
- **Actual before the fix:** every attempt installed `SettlementOverride = FinalPoints`; postponement changed it to `FinalPoints − cost`. `CombineRoundScoreAndGameScore` treated any override as a complete replacement, forced `ActualRoundMultiplier = 1`, discarded the normal multiplied ledger and awarded unrealized `P^P` even when release did not happen.
- **Fixed:** ordinary settlement is never overridden. Gordon now prepares a flat `SettlementAdjustment`: zero for freeze/failure, `−cost` for postponement, and `max(0, P^P − ordinarySettlement)` only on release. `InGameStatus` snapshots the adjustment as a flat HL3 entry while retaining the normal multiplier and applies it after ordinary score. The total on release remains `P^P`; round-5 `P=2` + first postpone is `2×2−1=3`, and round-5 `P=3` + release is `3×2+21=27`. Pre-decision Rumbling/Цукуеми projection is ordinary-only; if release happens, the recorded Itachi thief receives the extra release profit then, so Wait/Freeze cannot mint or later deduct imaginary profit (`GordonFreeman.PrepareHalfLifeSettlement`/`ResolveHalfLifeDecision`/`ReleaseHalfLife3`/`ProjectRegularSettlement`/`CreditReleaseProfitToItachi`; `InGameStatus.CombineRoundScoreAndGameScore`; `DoomsdayMachine.CompleteRoundAsync`).

### M175. Нож-паук committed leaps through solid obstacles

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** Нож-паук must understand that a solid obstacle blocks the ray to the player and reposition instead of jumping straight into it.
- **Actual before the fix:** the v2 leap gate swept only other living enemy circles. It ignored room obstacles, so a spider could begin an impossible jump and inevitably embed itself against the intervening prop.
- **Fixed:** `knifeSpiderLeapPathClear` now rejects every player ray intersecting an obstacle expanded by the spider's own radius before checking mob clearance. The ordinary chase continues until a clear leap exists; a targeted regression places the spider and player on opposite sides of a prop (`engine.ts` `knifeSpiderLeapPathClear`; `engine.spec.ts` obstacle-leap regression).

### M176. The 99LC prologue waited for combat and used a blocking fake-parry button

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** entering the prologue must immediately show an airborne Нож-паук approaching a stationary hero; time progressively slows and freezes at parry distance; an offset `Отбить (<left tap>)` instruction must not be a button or window; the real left tap reflects the spider and time smoothly returns.
- **Actual before the fix:** the room began in ordinary enemy idle/chase/wind-up state. At freeze, a full-screen blurred modal card obscured the arena and its dedicated HTML button—not the player's attack binding—triggered the parry.
- **Fixed:** room entry launches the v2 spider immediately from the first collision-clear 330-unit ray, player movement stays frozen through approach, simulation reaches zero at 112 units, and the real left-tap path for every selected scheme performs the reflection. The page renders only a pointer-transparent, side-offset status instruction; successful reflection retains the 900 ms slow-motion resume (`engine.ts` `startKnifeSpiderPrologue`/`knifeSpiderTutorialTimeScale`/`performAttack`; `pages/LastChances.vue` `.lc-spider-tutorial-hint`; prologue regression in `engine.spec.ts`).

### M177. Мать тараканов used an oversized rectangle and could strike from her entrance hole

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** the room is square and compact; the hole blast reaches practically all of it except the opposite corner; the strike comes only from the linked hole; all four holes are easy to memorize by both shape and color.
- **Actual before the fix:** the arena was 1488×837 with a 560-radius blast. Each retreat flipped a coin between the entrance and linked hole, and the four muted brown rims were difficult to distinguish despite their shape differences.
- **Fixed:** the room is 840×840 and Retreat always selects `entrance.linkedHoleId`; schema v10 removes `sameHoleChance`. Circle, square, triangle and diamond holes now also use distinct red, blue, green and gold colors, and validation rejects duplicate hole colors. The original 900-radius fix was expanded to 925 in the 2026-08-03 balance pass; M236 later separates the entrance-opposite safe corner from the seeded exit link (`game-config.json`; `config.ts` boss-hole validation/`backfillCockroachBalanceFields`; `engine.ts` `applyCockroachMotherHealthGate`).

### M178. Clearing rooms did not increase current-attempt stamina expenditure

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** every cleared room adds one icon beside stamina and +10% stamina expenditure, capped at ten icons / +100%.
- **Actual before the fix:** stamina costs were determined only by the authored attack (plus weapon fatigue), and no per-room attempt state or HUD indicator existed.
- **Fixed:** schema v10 authors `staminaCostIncreasePerRoom: 0.1` and `maxStaminaCostStacks: 10`. `finishRoomTransition` adds one capped stack; every normal action and each direct «Прорыв» tick pays the resulting ×1.1…×2 multiplier. The snapshot exposes count/multiplier and the HUD renders the corresponding warning icons and percentage beside stamina. Ordinary retry clears stacks; a paid boss checkpoint restores its exact boss-entry count (`types.ts`; `config.ts`; `engine.ts` `staminaCostMultiplier`/`finishRoomTransition`/`retryAttempt`; `pages/LastChances.vue` `.lc-stamina-attrition`).

### M179. Voluntary Chance costs postponed their stat loss until a later death

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** the existing “lose stats per five Chances spent on pickup/save/etc.” rule affects the current run immediately: paying five Chances means the current body has already lost those stats.
- **Actual before the fix:** altar saves, reward choices and Ouroboros pickups only subtracted Chances. Stat erosion was applied exclusively when the player later died, so the paid room continued with stale stronger current stats.
- **Fixed:** schema v10 authors `chanceErosionStep: 5`. All voluntary costs share a generation-persistent spent remainder; each complete five immediately applies the current tier's entire erosion vector to both `generationBaseStats` and current `player.stats`, clamping live HP, mind and stamina to their new maxima. Death costs keep their existing one-erosion-per-death path and do not enter the voluntary counter, avoiding double erosion (`engine.ts` `spendVoluntaryChances`/`applyStatErosion` and altar/interaction/Ouroboros callers; cumulative-cost regression in `engine.spec.ts`).

### M180. Battleship bot, fire, setup, Boarding and ramming contracts diverged

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** bot fleets/targets respect poison, Capture, Ballista dodge memory and the Иссушитель ban; Горючая баржа, Маневрирующая двойка and Пираты are common-faction ships; fire-resistant decks are black with both exact statuses for both viewers; the two evil weapons, Alliance assembling ship and revised Triple stats are purchasable. Setup permits four bow directions, per-Tetracatapult White Stone/Buckshot loadouts and undo before the opponent confirms. Mandatory Boarding deployment precedes a Cursed Boat course. Famous Ramming physically replaces allied decks without leaking them and later reveals the surviving sparse hull correctly.
- **Actual before the fix:** the bot could poison its own fleet, waste Capture/Ballista shots and draft Иссушитель; faction catalogs, fire-resistance presentation, purchases, loadouts, orientations and confirmation flow lacked those rules. Boarding and Cursed Boat locks could mutually deadlock. Famous Ramming left destroyed allied decks in the ship model and exposed their death/Space to the opponent. Scout itself was re-audited and was already cell-scoped rather than ship-wide.
- **Fixed:** `BattleshipBotAI` now enforces all four targeting/placement constraints. `ShipCatalog`, validators and engine add the common faction entries, aim-0 Горючка variants, evil upgrades, 2/4/2 Triple and assembling components; fire/explosion paths share `MarkFireResistance`. The service/hub expose exact per-weapon loadouts, reversible placement, response-window Evil Greek Fire, mandatory assembly and Boarding-first priority. Four-orientation sparse geometry is shared by engine and Vue; DTO-driven cells render the black resistance result, latest Light Wood marker, factions and stable sunk-ship name. Ramming removes the collided `Deck` and its weapon without dispatching a public death, while later `RevealShip` resolves the surviving deck indices (`ShipCatalog`; `PlacementValidator`; `FleetValidator`; `BattleshipGameEngine.MarkFireResistance`/`GetPoisonConeCells`/`ApplyManeuver`/`RevealShip`; `BattleshipService.ConfirmPlacement`/`CancelPlacement`/`GetPendingAssembly`/`AssembleShip`; `BattleshipGame.vue`; `PlacementPhase.vue`; `CombatPhase.vue`; `battleship-geometry.ts`).

### M181. СОсиновый кол could subtract the same lost bite repeatedly

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** each Вампур defeat removes one active Гематофагия bite and reverses exactly the +2 stat recorded by that bite. Once removed, a bite cannot consume any more stats.
- **Actual before the fix:** when the winner had no active bite, the removal queue selected a random bite but deduplicated it against the winner's ID instead of the selected bite's ID. Multiple losses in one round could therefore queue the same bite repeatedly; end-of-round settlement subtracted its recorded stat and −1 point for every stale queue entry even after the bite had already been removed, allowing bite cleanup to consume base stats down to zero.
- **Failure scenario:** Вампур holds one bite, then loses two or more fights in the same round to enemies without bites. Every loss selects the only bite, so its recorded stat is reduced by 4/6/etc. although only one +2 bite bonus ever existed.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — each loss chooses only among active bites not already queued, preferring the winner's bite; settlement first proves and removes that exact active bite, then reverses its recorded `StatIndex` once and charges one regular point. Stale entries cannot alter stats or score (`CharacterPassives` `Гематофагия` after-fight/end-of-round cases).

### M182. Battleship's bot pump serialized the defender's summon response behind the barrage

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** during the reset response window, a human defender can deploy a summon immediately between any two enemy shots, including against a bot.
- **Actual before the fix:** every action that handed control to a bot awaited `RunBattleshipBotPump` inside the originating SignalR hub invocation. SignalR serialized the same connection's next invocation behind that unfinished action, so a defender's `BattleshipDeploySummon` request commonly reached the server only after the bot's retained-shot chain ended even though the per-game lock was released during each delay.
- **Failure scenario:** miss against a bot, then try to deploy during the bot's first eight-second reset window; the summon appears only after the bot has finished its barrage.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — every hub action now queues the bot continuation after returning its initiating SignalR invocation instead of awaiting the complete barrage. The pump keeps the one-step/per-game guard and exact delay, but background pushes use `IHubContext<GameHub>`; a deployment arriving from the same browser connection can therefore execute during the released response window and request pump continuation afterward (`GameHub.QueueBattleshipBotPump`/`RunBattleshipBotPump`/Battleship push helpers).

### M183. Final Boarding duplicated converted Close ships instead of moving them

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28, superseding the earlier I02 rejection):** each eligible Close/Close melee ship leaves its original cells and becomes one mandatory boarding ship on the enemy board. It has no second copy or active source-board passives; when the boarding unit dies, that ship and its remaining weapon source die with it.
- **Actual before the fix:** `QueueBoardingShipsForMidlessPlayers` only set `Ship.IsSummon` and queued a generic Ram. The original live ship, decks, modules and cells stayed on the owner's board and continued participating in victory/weapon accounting, while the bot-deployed unit looked like an ordinary summon.
- **Failure scenario:** trigger Boarding for a bot with living Close ships; generic Ram icons appear on the enemy board while the same Close hulls remain alive in their original cells and can prolong the match after their boarding copies die.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — conversion snapshots the eligible Close/Close melee list, clears each source cell and removes the hull from `PlacedShips` before creating the mandatory deployment. Pending/living Boarding units prevent premature fleet-destruction loss; the fleet record retains only stable identity and a deployed Close Ballista source, then `MarkBoardingSourceDestroyed` kills it when the unit dies. The DTO carries the source name and Boarding flag so the client renders a ship rather than a generic Ram. Bot steps give all mandatory Boarding placements global priority (`BattleshipGameEngine.QueueBoardingShipsForMidlessPlayers`/`GetControlledShips`/`CheckFleetDestructionWin`/`MarkBoardingSourceDestroyed`; `BattleshipService.ProcessBotStep`/DTO mapping; `CellComponent.vue`).

### M184. CAPTURE removed every ammunition choice except Ballista

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** while CAPTURE remains the only legal target, the original owner may select any otherwise operational ammunition and spend it against that captured ship.
- **Actual before the fix:** `SelectWeapon`, `ValidateSelectedWeapon`, `MapAvailableWeapons`, `HasAnyLegalShot` and `ProcessShot` all hard-coded Ballista-only capture handling. The Vue weapon bar therefore had no other server-provided choice.
- **Failure scenario:** keep a loaded Tetracatapult or fire weapon when a ship is captured; the player sees only Ballista and cannot choose that ammunition before firing at the mandatory own-board target.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — CAPTURE filtering was removed from weapon validation, selection and DTO mapping while its coordinate restriction remains in `ProcessShot`. `ProcessCapturedShipShot` spends the selected weapon, applies its configured damage/fire/module effect and always ends the turn. `HasAnyLegalShot` and bot `ChooseWeapon` use every operational, loaded and aimed source, so both human and bot can choose ammunition without escaping the mandatory own-board target (`BattleshipGameEngine.HasAnyLegalShot`/`ProcessShot`/`ProcessCapturedShipShot`; `BattleshipService.SelectWeapon`/`ValidateSelectedWeapon`/`MapAvailableWeapons`; `BattleshipBotAI.ChooseWeapon`/`ChooseTarget`).

### M185. Drakkar-frozen summons had no freeze presentation

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** a summon caught by a Drakkar shows the freeze animation and retains a freeze badge on its individual death marker.
- **Actual before the fix:** `FreezeSummon` wrote the same unqualified `SummonDeaths` entry as every other death. The DTO had no frozen-death metadata, so `diffBoardAnimations` could not trigger freeze VFX and `CellComponent` rendered only the ordinary summon-death icon.
- **Failure scenario:** deploy or move any summon into Drakkar Space; it disappears with an ordinary death marker and no freeze animation or snowflake badge.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — `Cell.FrozenSummonDeathIndices` records which entry in the ordered `SummonDeaths` list came from Drakkar; both owner/fog DTOs preserve the parallel order. The board diff now triggers freeze animation/VFX/sound on a newly added frozen death, while `CellComponent` gives only that marker an icy style, persistent snowflake badge and “Заморожен” tooltip (`BattleshipGameEngine.FreezeSummon`/`MarkSummonDeath`; `BattleshipService.MapBoard`/`MapFogBoard`; `battleship.ts.diffBoardAnimations`; `CellComponent.vue`).

### M186. TheBoys roll weight ignored every СУПЕР except Homelander

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-28):** every СУПЕР already fixed in the roster doubles TheBoys' roll weight. Homelander is one such СУПЕР and additionally increases the resulting weight by 500%.
- **Actual before the fix:** both natural and draft rolls tested only a `homelanderInRoster` boolean and applied ×6. Сайтама, Omni-man and the other canonical roster Supers contributed nothing; Homelander also missed the ordinary ×2 owed by his Super classification (`StartGameLogic.cs` pre-fix `HandleCharacterRoll`/`RollDraftOptions`).
- **Failure scenario:** with Сайтама and Omni-man already in the lobby, TheBoys retained baseline weight 80 instead of 320. With Homelander alone, the weight was 480 instead of `80 × 2 × 6 = 960`.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-28 — one shared roster multiplier now applies ×2 for each distinct canonical roll-time Super and then Homelander's separate ×6. Natural rolls count guaranteed reservations/forced line-ups up front and newly random Supers only after assignment; draft alternatives count the complete assigned roster. Tier pity and Store multipliers remain later layers (`StartGameLogic.GetTheBoysRosterRollMultiplier`/`TheBoysRollMultiplierPerSuper`/`HomelanderTheBoysRollMultiplier`; `HandleCharacterRoll`/`RollDraftOptions`).

### M187. Глаз Шусуи created a real death before reviving Итачи

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** Изанаги from **Глаз Шусуи** prevents Итачи's death. It is not a death event and must not pay or advance Кира, Монстр, Эрен, Кратос or any other death-dependent reward/counter.
- **Actual before the fix:** every lethal source first marked Итачи dead, emitted its death log and paid all source/Монстр rewards. `Глаз Шусуи` revived him only when the next round opened, so the temporary death was indistinguishable from a real death to every earlier observer.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — every real lethal branch calls one immediate `Itachi.TryPreventDeath` interceptor before death state, logs, rewards and trackers. The one Shisui use is consumed and revealed, but `IsDead`, `DeathSource`, `WasRevived` and all source counters remain untouched. The stale next-round branch is only a compatibility fallback for already persisted in-flight death state (`Itachi.TryPreventDeath`; `CharacterPassives` Kira/Kratos/Rumbling/Monster/pitchfork/L-arrest kill branches).

### M188. Correct Death Note names could be reused after survival or resurrection

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** Кира may write each target at most once. A correct name remains spent if Джон Сноу survives through **Мой дозор окончен**, Итачи prevents death through Изанаги, or the target is otherwise immune; the notebook wording uses «стереть», not «удалить».
- **Actual before the fix:** `FailedTargets` recorded only wrong names. Every correct target that remained alive could be selected and written again on a later round.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — the historical DTO field now means every used target and is written before resolving the notebook outcome, including glass fizzles, immunity and death interception. Web and bot target gates already consume that set, so neither can select the target again. Entries separately record `CausedDeath`, preventing a correct-but-prevented name from advancing Kira's kill achievements. The supplied phrase is now `Стереть... Стереть... Стереть!` (`Kira.DeathNoteClass`/`DeathNoteEntry`; `CharacterPassives` `Тетрадь смерти`; `AchievementService.TrackCharacterAchievements`; `CharactersPhrases`).
- **Rework follow-up, 2026-08-08:** “used target” now begins only after a new interruption gate. `CaptureActiveLForRound` freezes any Шэн-redirection result before the simultaneous fight batch. Before glass RNG, name comparison, `FailedTargets` or history, the pending write is cancelled when the selected victim's final queue attacks Kira (fight/win unnecessary) or that captured active L won any resolved fight against Kira from either direction. The sole owner phrase is `Черт, не могу записать его имя, пока он так пристально смотрит... Надо попробовать еще раз`; it reveals no correctness, records no attempt and makes the target immediately eligible on the next turn. All writes that pass the gate retain M188's permanent single-use rule. The same rework limits every guesser to one active `Кира` row across manual/bot/earned paths, atomically synchronizing bot `PredictionEvidence`; serializes web/Discord prediction mutations with their authoritative action-window checks and keeps every mutable Confirm precheck plus sheet snapshot under the game lock; atomically resets eligible humans and opens the action window before UI publication, then closes it under that same lock at readiness cutoff; distinguishes explicit-human/strict-bot-round-8 final confirmation from death/timeout/no-sheet readiness booleans, using `PlayerType` so a living disconnected human is not treated as a strict bot while a dead human remains auto-ready without evidence; and snapshots L-arrest qualification once at opening round 9. A dead-before-Confirm L with a correct-at-death row needs confirmed-correct unanimity from every other living seat except Kira and original L (at least one; revived L stays excluded; no Madara/Булькает/no-sheet capability exemption), while a previously confirmed dead L uses the ordinary rule. Jon-as-L is captured directly inside `JonSnow.TryEndWatch` before revival. After all opening resurrection hooks, `ResolveKiraArrests` qualifies the whole Kira batch and snapshots living Монстр recipients against one survivor state before applying any deaths, making voter pools and per-death Monster payouts iteration-order independent. Qualification remains armed after Изанаги prevents an attempt (`Kira.CaptureActiveLForRound`/`IsCurrentDeathNoteEntryInterrupted`/`SetPrediction`/`TryFinalizePredictionConfirmation`/`CaptureLDeathPredictionState`/`IsArrestQualified`; `JonSnow.TryEndWatch`; `CharacterPassives.ResolveKiraArrests`; `CheckIfReady.HandleCompletedRound`/`TickAsync`).

### M189. Monster priority overrode the mandatory round-10 bot attack on Eren

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** on round 10, every actionable bot below a selectable Эрен Йегер must attack him. This is an absolute action priority over Monster and every ordinary character plan.
- **Actual before the fix:** `TryForceRoundTenBossAttack` always selected a roster Monster first and considered Eren only when no Monster existed.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — the scripted selector first chooses selectable Eren above the bot and only then falls back to Monster. Existing unable-to-act, forced-Skip, sealed and round-10-ban gates remain authoritative; Dopa Macro still appends its required second action (`BotsBehavior.TryForceRoundTenBossAttack`).
- **Superseded by designer request, 2026-08-08:** the Eren-first/real-Monster fallback selector above was retained as history but temporarily stopped defining play. Round 10 instead required every actionable strict bot at AI level 0–4 to submit an attack against its own highest-ranked selectable Monster suspect from viewer-scoped evidence, without reading the real target name/passives or actual roster. Exact non-Monster reveals ranked below unresolved seats, equal hypotheses were randomized, and rejected candidates were retried until a legal attack was accepted or none existed. Genuine unable-to-act states and later authoritative forced/redirect/sealing/ban/Dopa-Skip/Цукуеми/Kratos layers remained above the ordinary intent (`CharacterPassives.RankRoundTenMonsterSuspects`).
- **Restored by designer request, 2026-08-09:** the exact Eren reaction is again the first round-10 global bot mandate: every actionable strict bot below a selectable Eren attacks him before the viewer-scoped Monster ranking. The Monster task remains the fallback and supplies Dopa's next distinct Macro target. Both now run from the version-independent designer-rule overlay (`BotsBehavior.TryApplyGlobalDesignerActionRules`/`TryForceRoundTenDesignerAttack`; M247).

### M190. Вампуризм granted even-round Moral after that round's fights

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** active bites grant their Moral at the start of rounds 2/4/6/8/10 so Вампур can spend it during that turn.
- **Actual before the fix:** the migrated dispatcher ran from `HandleEndOfRound`, after the even round's actions and fights. Вампур received the correct amount one action window too late.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — the even-round grant runs from `HandleNextRound` when the even action round opens and reads the then-active bite list. The obsolete end-of-round branch was removed (`CharacterPassives` `Вампуризм` next-round case).

### M191. An expired Francie order charged the following round

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** a completed order pays +1 in its completion round; an uncompleted three-turn order charges −1 in its final eligible round, using that same round's multiplier. Replacements open on rounds 4 and 7.
- **Actual before the fix:** completion already paid in the correct fight hook, but failure was processed only after `RoundNo++` in `HandleNextRound`. The first two failed orders therefore entered rounds 4/7 and used the following round's ledger/multiplier; the last failed order was charged after round-10 settlement.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — unresolved orders expire in `HandleEndOfRound` on rounds 3/6/9 before score settlement. The next-round branch now only draws replacements on rounds 4/7 (`CharacterPassives` `Francie` end-of-round/next-round cases).

### M192. Ziggurat used the next-round position and charged after sorting

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** blocking captures the Goblins' current leaderboard cell. The Ziggurat is built on that cell and its −3 points/−1 Worker cost is charged in the blocked round, before settlement and the next sort.
- **Actual before the fix:** Block stored only a boolean. Construction, affordability, payment and place lookup ran in `HandleNextRoundAfterSorting`, so the new leaderboard position selected the cell and the price landed a full transition late.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — every web, Discord, bot and auto-move Block path captures the submission position. `HandleEndOfRound` resolves construction/payment at that captured cell before score settlement; the post-sort hook only checks whether the new position stands on an already-built Ziggurat and applies its next-round benefits (`GoblinSwarm.RecordZigguratBuildIntent`; `CharacterPassives.ResolveGoblinZigguratBuild`; `WebGameService.Block`; `GameReaction` Block paths).

### M193. Monster's two-turn no-escape mark allowed empty or invalid actions

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** while the mark is active, a player with at least one legal enemy must attack. Block, Skip, an empty queue or a stale/illegal target cannot consume the turn.
- **Actual before the fix:** the readiness rewrite ran only when `IsBlock` or `IsSkip` was already true. An empty/invalid ready action could pass through untouched, including the reported Tiger Block outcome after other action layers mutated the flags.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — every active marked victim is revalidated after all ordinary action layers. Illegal targets are removed, one legal target is injected when necessary, Block/Skip is cleared and readiness is committed. A genuine no-legal-target state plus existing unknown_bug, sealed, round-10-ban and Kratos/Цукуеми exclusions remain authoritative (`CheckIfReady.TickAsync` Monster forced-action layer).

### M194. Correct predictions stopped scoring when the guessed target died

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** correctness depends on the submitted character identity, not whether the guessed target survives. A living guesser receives the point/bonus for every correct admissible target, including a target that later died.
- **Actual before the fix:** ordinary final scoring, M.M.'s Компромат count and AWDKA's Троллинг bonus all required `!target.Passives.IsDead`. Admin sheet completion also skipped dead seats.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — those four paths resolve identity against dead or living admissible targets alike. The source guesser must still be eligible/living, and Sakura, unknown_bug, fictional entries and Naruto sibling exclusions remain unchanged (`CheckIfReady.HandleLastRound` and prediction preparation).

### M195. OPEN — Goblin level-up population bonuses refresh only at the next transition

- **Audit question (designer report, 2026-07-29):** should **Контрактная армия** and **Праздник** affect fights immediately after the level-up, or only after the next round boundary?
- **Current behavior:** the action mutates Warrior/Worker/Hob rates or doubles `TotalGoblins` immediately, but the Warrior Skill delta and population-derived Str/Int/Psyche are applied only by the later `Гоблины` `HandleNextRoundAfterSorting` case. Fights in the level-up round therefore use the old Warrior multiplier and old population stat baseline.
- **Decision needed:** choose immediate level-up recomputation or explicitly preserve next-transition activation. No silent change was made because the desired timing changes the strength of every Goblin upgrade (`GameReactions` Goblin custom level-ups; `CharacterPassives.ApplyGoblinPopulationStats`; `GoblinPopulation.AppliedWarriorSkillBonus`).
- **Related transition audit:** fixed in this batch: M190 Vampur Moral, M191 Francie expiry and M192 Ziggurat cell/payment. Verified already current-round/immediate: Francie completion and Sirinoks training completion. Verified intentionally available on the following action round: a Vampur bite's stat/Justice consequences settle after the winning fight batch. The broader unresolved same-batch output/resource families remain catalogued under M171.

### M196. Battleship Boarding, moved-deck reconnaissance and summon recovery diverged from the reported rules

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** converted ships retain source-specific identity and Scout-style deferred Space; every available summon/Brander and each living Crew Triple's manually column-restricted Pirate Boat is an immediate mandatory Boarding action, capped at ten, while bowless ships stay home and boarding Rams can turn back. Incendiary variants return to a surviving Ballista, the Evil upgrade replaces normal Incendiary, assembly removes dead-component marks, and reconnaissance relocates exact moved-deck markers. Captured Incendiary Barges explode on first contact; waiting Rams block other summon actions; Pirate Boats capture Devastated enemies or fully repair an allied Devastated ship; direct Incendiary Scout kills suppress its payload. Intact moved decks outrank old miss paint and fleet range `Close` remains literal.
- **Actual before the fix:** converted units collapsed to generic Ram labels in the active/trail/death projections, revealed continuously, died at the far edge and were the only mandatory Boarding deployments. Crew auto-picked a column, unused summon/Brander entitlements remained optional, no ten-unit overflow/bow gate existed, and marker records discarded source identity. Fire selection and the Evil upgrade retained the wrong weapons; assembly/moved-hull fog retained stale or suppressed marks. CAPTURE disabled the Barge explosion, Devastated hulls could not collide with Pirates, own-board Incendiary Scout kills were free and leaked reconnaissance, and the client painted stale misses/translated `Close`.
- **Failure scenario:** enter Boarding with two living Singles, unused regional summons, a purchased Brander and a Crew Triple; the pause required only two generic Rams, auto-spawned one Pirate in an unrelated column and allowed combat before the remaining units were committed. A converted Single revealed every step immediately, could not return at the far edge and left only `Таран` history.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — identity-preserving summon markers, deferred reveal payloads and exact-ID Ram return now cover converted hulls; one generalized mandatory-deployment state machine snapshots ordinary/Brander entitlements, Crew columns and a ten-placement capacity, discarding overflow sources permanently and excluding a missing/dead deck index 0. Weapon lifecycle, assembly cleanup and cell-scoped known-deck relocation implement the fire/assembly/Scout/ramming rules. Explicit Devastated→Capture/full-repair transitions, ammo capacities and the captured-Barge exception complete Pirate/fire behavior. SignalR/Vue expose the new actions and markers, preserve frozen-death identity, prioritize intact moved decks and opt the range enum out of DOM localization (`BattleshipGameEngine`; `BattleshipService`; `ShipCatalog`; `GameHub`; `BattleshipModels`; `BattleshipBotAI`; Battleship Vue components/store/SignalR contracts).

### M197. A provenance-only Empire catalog correction invalidated authentic v17/v18 active-minigame saves

- **Expected:** an authentic Empire's Endgame v17/v18 save may resume its active minigame against the exact current bundled successor configuration. Historical identities remain pinned, and stale or custom configurations remain rejected.
- **Actual before the fix:** correcting OrgАн's `sourceMessageIds` changed the current TD, Alchemy, Inventory and Clash aggregate rules digests, but `AUTHENTIC_BUNDLED_CURRENT_RULES_DIGESTS` still pinned their pre-correction successors. Loading an otherwise authentic legacy save therefore failed with `Active minigame rules identity does not match the loaded configuration`; Tavern happened to remain valid because its aggregate did not include the corrected Clash roster.
- **Failure scenario:** restore the checked-in authentic v17 TD fixture while the corrected current bundle is loaded; the legacy identity is accepted, but its now-stale successor pin rejects the current TD configuration before play can resume.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — regenerated and pinned the four affected current successor digests. Historical v17/v18 input identities, Tavern's unchanged successor, custom-rule rejection and all gameplay/configuration data remain unchanged (`engine.ts` `AUTHENTIC_BUNDLED_CURRENT_RULES_DIGESTS`; `phase14.spec.ts` authentic legacy identity gate).

### M198. The legacy English catalog was incomplete for recent character batches

- **Expected:** `tools/audit-localization.sh` covers every canonical character/passive sheet and display name, and every value in `DataBase/localization.en.json` is usable English presentation.
- **Actual before the fix:** the phrase catalog passed (253 groups / 834 variants), but the legacy sheet audit stopped first on missing character entries for Homelander and Omni-man. The complete inventory also found 14 missing passive descriptions, 19 missing character/passive display-name mappings, the later ScamRat `Сделка` phrase title and fallback, and 12 English-side values that still contained Cyrillic on the Cthulhu surface.
- **Impact:** the structured `Localization/*.messages.json` path introduced in v5.1.55 is complete for new shell/product copy, but these pre-existing character sheets can still fall back to Russian while ENG is selected. Exact English player-facing adaptations are required; canonical Russian keys and the authored Russian descriptions must remain untouched (`tools/audit-localization.sh`; `DataBase/characters.json`; `DataBase/localization.en.json`; `docs/LOCALIZATION.md` §3.2).
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — added source-faithful English for all 14 missing passive descriptions, retained empty English biographies for the two empty Russian Homelander/Omni-man biographies, completed all 20 missing display/fallback mappings and replaced all 12 Russian-valued Cthulhu compatibility entries. The reversed Necronomicon clue stays reversed in English and the corrupted horror line retains Latin-base distortion. `characters.json` and `phrases.en.json` remain byte-for-byte unchanged, and every canonical Russian key/text remains verbatim; `tools/audit-localization.sh` now passes 47 characters, 216 passives and 132 phrase classes (`DataBase/localization.en.json`; `docs/LOCALIZATION.md` §3.2).

### M199. Страх перед Мадарой appeared in the following turn's PTS presentation

- **Expected:** an enemy who completes round 8 without a resolved fight against Madara pays the hidden −5 bonus-point penalty in the results of that same round.
- **Actual before the fix:** `ResolveRoundNine` applied the penalty from `HandleNextRound`, after round 8 had already settled and `ReplayService.CaptureRoundResult` had frozen its score presentation. The score changed only after `RoundNo++`, so the PTS feed attributed it one turn late.
- **Failure scenario:** ignore the round-8 Клоны Сусано challenge, then skip the fight replay → round-8 PTS contains no `Страх перед Мадарой`; the −5 appears with round 9 instead.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-30 — `ApplyRoundEightFear` runs once at the start of `CompleteRoundAsync`, before score settlement and replay/result capture, and carries an idempotent round-8 state latch. `ResolveRoundNine` still owns only the later dialogue, Red Tiger, sealing and ending decisions (`Madara.ApplyRoundEightFear`; `DoomsdayMachine.CompleteRoundAsync`; `Madara.ResolveRoundNine`).
