- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — the canonical RU and EN catalogs now store the exchange as one newline-paired variant, guaranteeing both lines and preserving replay localization (`CharactersPhrases.TheBoysVirusApply`; `phrases.en.json`).

### m57. `c_goblin_summit` unlocked without the Ziggurat ever enforcing the win
- **Expected:** the legendary Goblin card *Built Different / Особая постройка* says "получите его гарантированную победу", so it must unlock only when the Ziggurat actually converted a loss into a win — i.e. `HandleLastRound`'s bonus-point overtake fired because the Goblins were not 1st after the final score sort.
- **Actual before the fix:** `TrackGameEnd` re-derived the condition from persistent state as `BuiltPositions.Contains(1) && rewardWin && actualPlace == 1`. `BuiltPositions.Contains(1)` stays true for the rest of the match once a place-1 Ziggurat exists, and the enforcement block itself is gated on `GetPlaceAtLeaderBoard() != 1` — so a Goblin leading on score never triggered it yet still unlocked the card (`AchievementClass.cs` pre-fix `TrackGameEnd` goblin block; `CheckIfReady.cs` `HandleLastRound`).
- **Failure scenario:** build a Ziggurat at place 1 in an early round, stay ahead on score to round 10; the overtake never runs and no `Гоблины построили Зиккурат на вершине!` log appears, but the legendary card (228 ZBS + 2 boxes) unlocks anyway.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — `ZigguratClass.EnforcedWinTriggered` is set inside the overtake branch in `HandleLastRound`, and `c_goblin_summit` is gated on that flag instead of `BuiltPositions.Contains(1)`. `rewardWin && actualPlace == 1` is retained because `Cthulhu.ApplyHeraldFinalPlacement`/`JonSnow.HandleFinalPosition` run after the overtake. Win behaviour is unchanged, and already-unlocked accounts are deliberately left alone (no migration or revocation) (`GoblinSwarm.cs` `ZigguratClass`; `CheckIfReady.cs` `HandleLastRound`; `AchievementClass.cs` `TrackGameEnd`).
- **Follow-up:** M205–M206 retain the explicit `EnforcedWinTriggered` achievement gate, but now set it only when a new round-10 summit visit's score-free first-place placement changes the natural result.

### m58. Итачи's armed crow was not placed when the attacked target blocked or skipped
- **Expected (designer note, 2026-07-25):** «Вороны должны накладываться даже если бой не состоялся» — an armed crow lands on the attacked enemy even when the fight never happens (target blocked or skipped).
- **Actual before the fix:** the `case "Вороны"` placement in `HandleCharacterAfterFight` resolved its target only from `IsWonThisCalculation`/`IsLostThisCalculation`, which are set exclusively for a resolved fight (`DoomsdayMachine.cs:1262-1263`, `1476-1477`). The hook *is* invoked on the block path (`DoomsdayMachine.cs:841`) and skip path (`DoomsdayMachine.cs:889`) with `attack == true`, but both fields are empty there, so no crow was placed; the armed-crow flag survived to the next attack.
- **Failure scenario:** Итачи levels up (crow armed), attacks an enemy who blocks → global log «Бой не состоялся (Блок)», no crow appears on the target; the crow only lands on a later resolved fight.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-25 — the crow target resolution falls back to `Status.IsTargetBlocked` / `Status.IsTargetSkipped` (the «Доебаться» pattern), so a blocked/skipped attack places the crow; unknown_bug guard and charge retention on a failed placement unchanged (`CP:3406-3431`).

### m59. Omni-man's private feedback omitted phrase sources and the active Стражи Земли target

- **Expected (designer note, 2026-07-26):** supplied **Стражи Земли** and **Частица нашей силы** phrases identify their source, mentioned enemy nicknames are visually emphasized, and Omni-man sees which player is currently losing a turn to his Guardians.
- **Actual before the fix:** both phrase families were appended as bare text, Guardians interpolated an unformatted nickname, the successful **Подумай, Марк!** reply likewise used an unformatted enemy source, and neither web nor Discord leaderboards projected the active Guardians sleep.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — private lines now carry exact `Стражи Земли:` / `Частица нашей силы:` prefixes and bold enemy names. Omni-man alone receives a `😴` marker on the currently scheduled enemy, but only while the copied Gleb sleep is actually active; Portal Gun prevention and Premade cancellation therefore suppress the icon. Web uses `PlayerDto.OmniManGuardiansAsleep`, while Discord derives the same marker server-side (`OmniMan.HandleIntelligenceWin`/`HandleEnemyAttack`/`ApplyGuardiansOfTheGlobe`/`IsSleepingFromGuardians`; `GameStateMapper.MapPlayer`; `GameUpdateMess.CustomLeaderBoardAfterPlayer`).

### m60. Братишка could make Предположения on the web, as a bot, and through a forged Discord interaction

- **Expected:** **Булькает** reads «Не умеет играть и использовать *Скилл*, *Мораль* и Предположения» (`characters.json`; `localization.en.json`). The Skill/Мораль half is enforced in shared logic (`CharacterClass.cs:1067,1123,1269`), so the prediction half must hold on every path too.
- **Actual before the fix:** the rule existed only as a disabled Discord component — `GetPredictMenu` set `WithDisabled(true)` and placeholder `Бууууууль` (`GameUpdateMess.cs:1677-1681`) and nothing else checked the passive. `WebGameService.Predict` gated Madara/Let's Roll/Sakura but not Булькает, and `Leaderboard.vue` `canPredict` excluded only Kira, so a web Братишка predicted freely and `CheckIfReady.cs:322-340` paid the bonus. `HandleBotPredict` skipped only Kira and Madara, so bot Братишка scored prediction points a human Братишка could not. `HandlePredic2` had no check either, so a forged `predict-2` interaction was accepted. Separately, round 8 reset `ConfirmedPredict` for him, forcing a meaningless confirm click that also gated his Discord attack menu (`GameUpdateMess.cs:1597-1601`).
- **Failure scenario:** play Братишка in the web client, predict every opponent correctly, collect +1 bonus per hit at game end — points the character is defined not to have access to.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-26 — `WebGameService.Predict` returns `Бууууууль`, `GameReactions.HandlePredic2` returns early, `HandleBotPredict` skips the holder without clearing `Predict` (`CP:7730-7736`), and round 8 auto-confirms Булькает next to Тетрадь смерти (`CheckIfReady.cs:1113-1116`). The web leaderboard renders a disabled «Бууууууль» button per opponent row and drops the round-8 confirm button (`Game.vue` `hasBulkaet`; `Leaderboard.vue` `canPredict`). The gate is the passive, matching Discord, so it follows mylorik's transform, Ктулху's «Культ» adept pick and a Goblin Ziggurat that learns Булькает. The scoring loop (`CheckIfReady.cs:322-340`) is deliberately unchanged: entries can now only exist if they were made *before* the holder acquired the passive, and voiding those retroactively would delete legitimately earned points.

### m61. Dead players retained stale Drop counters and re-entered the position mover

- **Expected:** dead seats do not participate in later position mechanics.
- **Actual before the fix:** the pre-fight reset skipped dead players, while the end-of-round Quality Drop selection did not exclude them. A dead seat could retain `StrengthQualityDropTimes` and be pushed again on later rounds.
- **Fixed:** the reset clears the counter for every seat, and the Drop mover independently filters `Passives.IsDead`. The second guard protects against any future stale counter source (`CheckIfReady.TickAsync`; `DoomsdayMachine` Quality Drop block).

### m62. Импакт overwrote rather than stacked other kite sources

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** every kite source stacks; Импакт contributes +2.
- **Actual before the fix:** `GetSpeedQualityKiteBonus` first accumulated the ordinary/passive value and Speed-10 flag, then assigned `2` for Импакт, erasing everything already present.
- **Fixed:** Импакт now adds 2 to the accumulated total. Its separate flat Speed-resist value 6 is unchanged (`CharacterClass.GetSpeedQualityKiteBonus`).

### m63. Штормяк's Doomsday Machine intervention was invisible and incompletely replayed

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** when the chaotic cat applies its ±5 fight trick, players should visibly understand that Штормяк touched the machine and may have flipped the result.
- **Actual before the fix:** the server changed weighing and redirected the point but the fight payload carried no durable appearance/delta/flip receipt, so Fight Animation and replay could not distinguish the intervention from ordinary math.
- **Fixed:** `FightEntryDto` stores `StormAppeared`, signed `StormWeighingDelta` and `StormFlipped`; replay persistence retains all three. Participant projections receive the exact delta/flip, while the public scope exposes only appearance. At the Step-1 result, all Fight Arena layouts render a labeled DOOMSDAY MACHINE paw slap, sparks and a 450 ms jolt, followed by a perspective-correct helped/hurt receipt and flip stamp; reduced-motion/skip uses a static receipt. «Все бои» retains `🐾`/`🐾↻` (`DoomsdayMachine` Storm fight-entry mapping; `ReplayService`; `GameStateMapper`; `FightArena.vue`/`FightArenaCards.vue`/`FightArenaClassic.vue`; `FightAnimation.vue`).

### m64. A round with no personal fights never released the PTS presentation gate

- **Expected:** score earned in a round is animated after that round's personal fight presentation. An empty personal-fight list is a completed presentation, not an infinite wait.
- **Actual before the fix:** `FightAnimation` returned from its replay watcher when `myFights` was empty and never emitted `replay-ended`, so `PlayerCard` never started the structured PTS feed. Content-only snapshots also meant two consecutive empty-fight rounds with identical logs/breakdowns could fail even after a one-shot empty emit.
- **Fixed:** empty replays receive the same round/perspective fingerprint, clear stale playback state and emit completion on the next Vue tick. `Game.vue` stores replay completion against a game/round/finish presentation key, so each new projection becomes pending; `PlayerCard` resets by a separate game/round score key plus breakdown content. Identical consecutive rounds therefore animate independently, while an unchanged round-11 live→finished projection cannot replay the same award (`FightAnimation.vue` replay-fingerprint watcher; `Game.vue` `presentationRoundKey`/`scorePresentationKey`; `PlayerCard.vue` `scoreAnimKey`).

### m65. Gordon's first Halflife 3 announcement disappeared before the next turn

- **Expected (designer verdict, 2026-07-27):** after Gordon announces for the first time, the next turn's public log begins with exact text `ВНИМАНИЕ! HilfLife3 был анонсирован!!!`.
- **Actual before the fix:** the action handler wrote a differently cased/spelled line into the current transient global buffer. Fight setup cleared that buffer, and no next-turn hook recreated it.
- **Fixed:** announcing stores a one-shot pending flag instead of logging immediately. The next `HandleNextRound` publishes the exact supplied line even if Kimiko has disabled Gordon's passives in the meantime, then consumes the flag so later turns cannot duplicate it (`GordonFreeman.AnnounceHalfLife3`/`PublishHalfLifeAnnouncementAtTurnStart`; `CharacterPassives.HandleNextRound`).

### m66. The Step-3 roll label could disagree with its needle or freeze at an interrupted position

- **Expected:** throughout the R3 roll, the printed percentage describes the needle's current position. Any final-only navigation (skip-to-end, fight thumbnail or replay deep link) snaps both to that fight's authoritative result.
- **Actual before the fix:** all three arenas positioned the needle with animated `r3NeedlePos` but printed final `r3RollPct` from the first frame. If final-only navigation interrupted the 850 ms roll, it also canceled the authoritative settlement timer without snapping the active animation, so a final `93.0%` label could remain near 69% on the bar (`FightAnimation.vue` pre-fix R3 watcher/navigation; `FightArena.vue`/`FightArenaCards.vue`/`FightArenaClassic.vue` pre-fix roll labels).
- **Failure scenario:** open a fight whose final roll is 93%, then press skip while the needle is crossing 69% → the final result renders a red needle at 69% labelled `93.0%`.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-27 — each arena prints the animated needle position, and the shared watcher also observes forced-final state plus selected-fight changes. It cancels the animation frame and marks the authoritative target settled before the final render, covering skip, thumbnail, `Все бои` row jump and replay deep link without changing R3 timing or outcome math (`FightAnimation.vue` R3 needle watcher; `FightArena.vue`/`FightArenaCards.vue`/`FightArenaClassic.vue` roll labels).
- **Live-update follow-up fixed:** 2026-07-29 — live SignalR projections replace `FightEntry` DTO objects even when their fight data is unchanged. The watcher observed the computed fight object by reference, so every projection reset the needle and queued another bounce; replay snapshots remained stable, and «Промотать» only masked the loop by forcing the final state. The watcher now uses a stable presentation fingerprint over the round, selected fight, perspective, participants, outcome and R3 values, plus a next-tick key guard. Identical DTO replacements therefore cannot restart the animation, while a real navigation or result change still can (`FightAnimation.vue` `r3PresentationKey` watcher; `r3-presentation-key.ts` `createR3PresentationKey`; `r3-presentation-key.spec.ts`).

### m67. OrgАн carried Настоятель Святой Церкви's Discord provenance

- **Expected:** OrgАн's `sourceMessageIds` points to Discord message `1369418274797781012`, whose exported text names OrgАн, describes the multi-barrel gun and says «Статы бы придумать».
- **Actual before the fix:** both the TypeScript roster and its public generated config pointed OrgАн to message `1382288986579664916`, which is the separate `7-7-7` Настоятель Святой Церкви card.
- **Failure scenario:** a provenance-driven roster audit follows OrgАн's source ID and attributes Настоятель's stats, passives and unfinished seven-kill sequence to the wrong unit.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — synchronized OrgАн's source to `1369418274797781012` in `Web/VueClient/src/features/empires-endgame/clash/catalog.ts` and `Web/VueClient/public/empires-endgame/game-config.json`; Настоятель retains its correct `1382288986579664916` source.

### m68. Standalone Clash could stall forever with survivors in disjoint columns

- **Expected:** every non-terminal Clash position can still progress toward elimination or a home-row breach.
- **Actual before the fix:** melee could advance only into a unit it had just killed. If the two surviving armies occupied different columns, neither side had a legal target or a fallen enemy cell to enter, so every later clash repeated only wait events.
- **Failure scenario:** a randomized `3×3` engine sweep left 121 of 200 otherwise valid games non-terminal after 40 clashes because their survivors had separated into non-interacting lanes.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — opposing melee fronts keep fighting across casualty-created gaps and a surviving melee occupies the fallen opposing front's cell. If the other side has no living unit anywhere in a column, its surviving front unit of any type marches exactly one adjacent empty cell; a kill claimant cannot move twice or attack again. Randomized verification completed all 200 fixed `3×3` games by clash 9 and all 200 varied `3…10 × 3…5` games by clash 13 (`ClashGameEngine.BuildIntents`/`BuildAdvancePairs`/`BuildUnopposedAdvanceClaims`/`ApplyUnopposedAdvances`).
- **Superseded by the 2026-08-08 designer correction:** findings M244–M246 remove through-gap attacks, remote-kill teleport and ranged default marching. Melee now advances one adjacent cell under the corrected death-fill/general-march rules. The original no-stall outcome is no longer an authorized reason to violate adjacency; contested same-cell melee claims and ranged-only disjoint columns remain the explicit open portion of M245.

### m69. Homelander's private Noir line hid and preceded Stan Edgar's public dismissal

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-07-29):** the public Stan Edgar threshold line, Homelander reply, defective-product line and missile volley appear in that order in the event log; Homelander then receives the additional private Noir line after the complete public exchange.
- **Actual before the fix:** the threshold line had no phrase marker and contained `70 очков`, so both live and replay parsers classified it as a gold score entry and filtered it out of the log panels. The client also merged personal logs before globals, placing the private Noir line before the three remaining public lines and making it appear to replace the missing threshold dialogue.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — the exact RU/EN threshold dialogue is classified as purple dialogue before the generic score rule. A shared presenter moves only the private Noir entry behind a complete threshold → Homelander → defective-product → volley sequence; incomplete or unrelated logs retain their original order in both live games and replays (`Game.vue` `parsePrevLogs`/`currentLogEntriesAll`; `Replay.vue` `parsePrevLogs`/`currentLogEntriesAll`; `log-presentation.ts` `isStanEdgarThresholdDialogue`/`orderStanEdgarDismissalLogs`; `log-presentation.spec.ts`).

### m70. A hard-coded TooStronk diagnostic leaked into a player's personal game log

- **Expected:** internal fight diagnostics stay in structured fight data and never appear in player-facing personal logs.
- **Actual before the fix:** `CalculateStep1` checked one hard-coded Discord account and appended `DEBUG: You tooSTONK ...` whenever that player was the enemy-side TooStronk participant in a logged fight.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — removed the player-facing debug branch. The ordinary `FightingData` diagnostics and `Step1Result` TooStronk fields remain available for fight presentation and troubleshooting (`CalculateRounds.CalculateStep1`).

### m71. Монстр's no-escape could revive Dopa's duplicate Макро turn long enough for Живое Оружие to erase it

- **Expected:** once Dopa selects the same target twice, Макро's absolute self-Skip remains authoritative for that round regardless of later forced-action layers or passive shutdown; no outgoing or incoming fight may execute.
- **Actual before the fix:** Монстр's late no-escape enforcement cleared the submitted Skip and injected an attack. The first Макро reassertion ran only after `TheBoys.DisablePassivesBeforeFights` and required Dopa still to own Макро, so a simultaneous Живое Оружие attack could erase the passive while Dopa temporarily looked actionable. The later check then skipped him, leaving both the revived action and otherwise forbidden incoming fights alive.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — `DuplicateTargetSkipRound` is the authoritative marker after activation and no longer depends on passive survival. `EnforceDuplicateTargetSkip` now runs after forced-action/contract expansion but before Живое Оружие, runs again after pre-calculation event expansion, and the fight loop treats that round marker as an unbreakable Skip (`Dopa.ActivateDuplicateTargetSkip`/`EnforceDuplicateTargetSkip`/`IsDuplicateTargetSkip`; `DoomsdayMachine.CalculateAllFights` preparation and Skip branches).

### m72. Dopa's earned Макро prediction auto-confirmed his entire round-8 sheet

- **Expected:** duplicate-target Макро inserts and protects only the identity row it earned. The player must still confirm the complete round-8 prediction sheet through the ordinary control.
- **Actual before the fix:** `ReassertMacroPrediction` also set `Status.ConfirmedPredict = true`. Selecting a duplicate target on round 8 therefore confirmed every other current/stale row and could satisfy readiness or Madara's clone-injection gate without the player's explicit sheet confirmation.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — reassertion now removes/reinserts only the earned target row and never mutates `ConfirmedPredict`; later prediction passes cannot erase the earned row, but confirmation remains an independent player action (`Dopa.ReassertMacroPrediction`; `CheckIfReady.TickAsync` prediction/readiness gates).

### m73. Private Cthulhu fallback text entered the public browser localization bundle

- **Expected:** `BrowserCatalog: false` excludes Cthulhu's identity, passives, prompts and legacy fallback text from static Vue assets; authorized owner presentation is localized by the backend.
- **Actual before the fix:** `publicLocalizationPlugin` removed records only when a key or value contained a private character/passive term. Five Cthulhu-only records—including the reversed Necronomicon clue—contained no such term and therefore entered the generated bootstrap chunk. The depths heading and adept-action copy were also hard-coded in `Game.vue`, while the seven plain lines were misclassified as `phraseFallbacks` even though they were not passive-title fallbacks.
- **Fixed:** 2026-07-29 — the legacy English catalog now declares exact section/key exclusions under `browserPrivate`; Vite rejects unknown sections plus duplicate/stale exclusion keys and removes every listed record before serializing `virtual:public-localization`. All twelve Cthulhu prompt/action/tooltip/plain-log keys are `exact` backend translations and explicitly private, including the two already caught by term matching. The owner-only character/passive sheet and ritual copy now carry localized display text beside untouched canonical identifiers; the active Thing row/fight entries do the same for every player and locale-local spectator. Generated assets contain none of those private keys or values (`DataBase/localization.en.json` `browserPrivate`; `vite.config.ts` `publicLocalizationPlugin`; `GameStateMapper.ToDto`/`MapPlayer`/`ApplyPrivateCharacterDisplay`/`ApplyBoardEntityDisplay`; DTO `GameStateDto`/`PlayerDto`/`CharacterDto`/`PassiveDto`/`FightEntryDto`; `Game.vue`; `SkillsPanel.vue`; `Leaderboard.vue`; `FightAnimation.vue`; `docs/LOCALIZATION.md` §3.2).

### m74. An empty «Мои сражения» tab looked actionable and never yielded to Results

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-08-04):** after showing `Нет ваших боев в этом раунде`, Casual automatically opens `Итоги` within three seconds and dims `Мои сражения` so its empty state is visible before selection.
- **Actual before the fix:** the empty-replay watcher correctly released the score-presentation gate (m64) but left the selected tab unchanged indefinitely, while its header looked identical to a tab containing fights.
- **Failure scenario:** a player has no incoming or outgoing fight in the completed round → the center panel remains on the empty personal-replay message until the player manually discovers `Итоги`.
- **Fixed:** 2026-08-04 — the empty Casual tab receives a muted disabled state and a separate cancelable three-second timer while it is visible. The timer opens `Итоги` only if the tab is still selected and still empty; a fight arrival, stable round-identity change or unmount clears it. Pro (where the tab is absent) and terminal projections keep their existing navigation (`FightAnimation.vue` `hasEmptyPersonalFightTab`/`clearEmptyFightTabTimer`; m64).

### m75. Store exposed the hidden Tier −1 taxonomy

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-08-08):** secret characters that an account has already seen remain adjustable in Store, but Tier −1 itself is hidden; the public sequence goes directly from PRO to T1.
- **Actual before the fix:** `Store.vue` derived both its tier-filter tabs and every card badge from the raw server tier, producing a visible `T-1` tab and `T-1` badge as soon as the account had seen Sakura or Ктулху.
- **Fixed:** 2026-08-08 — public filters contain only nonnegative tiers and negative-tier cards omit both badge and tier-derived CSS class. Those cards remain available through «Все», search and the unchanged owner-only weight controls (`Store.vue`; `WebGameService.BuildStoreState`).

### m76. Претендент's settled ×3 was missing from the structured PTS receipt

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-08-08):** the score UI displays the actual regular points received under **Претендент русского сервера** and marks the summed delta with `(x3!)`; raw +4 therefore appears as `+12 (x3!)`.
- **Actual before the fix:** the passive multiplied only `ScoresToGiveAtEndOfRound`. The real score settled at +12, but `PreviousRoundScoreEntries` stayed at raw +4 and `ScoreBreakdown` carried only the ordinary ×1/×2/×4 multiplier, so the Vue animation and totals displayed +4.
- **Fixed:** 2026-08-08 — `MultiplyPendingRegularPoints` records the passive factor alongside the already-scaled aggregate. Settlement snapshots/reset it independently, the DTO exposes `RegularPointsMultiplier`, and Vue applies `roundMultiplier × regularPointsMultiplier` only to regular entries with legacy replay fallback ×1. The top summed delta appends `(x3!)`; bonus entries and the actual score are not multiplied again (`InGameStatus`; `CharacterPassives`; `GameStateMapper.MapStatus`; `score-combo.ts`; `PlayerCard.vue`).

### m79. Капитан Очевидность phrases disappeared from live and replay Events

- **Expected (designer report, 2026-08-09):** after the correct place-2 prediction transfers one point, the guesser sees `Капитан Очевидность: Вы правильно угадали лидера. Честь и хвала.`, and the guessed leader sees `Капитан Очевидность: Вас разоблачили, вы были слишком... очевидны.` as their respective personal Events.
- **Actual before the fix:** the point transfer first auto-logged a `Капитан Очевидность: ±1 ... очков` receipt, then the authored plain-text phrase used the same source prefix. `AddInGamePersonalLogs` coalesced those adjacent records, after which both live and replay `parsePrevLogs` classified the combined line as gold because it contained `очков` and removed it from Events. The structured score feed retained only the point entry, so the appended phrase had no visible surface.
- **Failure scenario:** place 2 correctly identifies place 1 at ordinary six-player settlement → the point is stolen and the achievements progress, but neither affected player can see the supplied Капитан Очевидность phrase in Events.
- **Fixed:** 2026-08-09 — each existing catalog phrase is now encoded as a separate bilingual owner-only payload. Its raw record has no mergeable source prefix, while owner projection restores the exact RU/EN text; the adjacent `±1` receipt remains an independent gold score record in live state and saved replay (`CheckIfReady.HandleCaptainObvious`/`AddCaptainObviousEvent`; `GameLocalization.MessageText`; `PhrasePayload.EncodeOwnerOnly`; `InGameStatus.AddInGamePersonalLogs`; `Game.vue`/`Replay.vue` `parsePrevLogs`).

The old request to rename `Тебя усыпили...` is superseded by D16: that exact phrase stays and is the standard future enemy-forced Skip notice. The current open set remains **M171, M195, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M236 separates Мать тараканов's entrance-opposite safe corner from the independently seeded linked strike/exit hole. The current open set remains **M171, M195, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M237 closes the 2026-08-08 Battleship overheat/Mast/Boarding/composite report and adds the two Ver.2 Alliance hulls plus its new default four-decker. The current open set remains **M171, M195, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M238 and m75–m76 close the 2026-08-08 HL3 audience, hidden Store tier and Претендент receipt report. The m30 follow-up restores the three canonical English gameplay labels without sacrificing the localized audio-control tooltip. The current open set remains **M171, M195, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M239–M243 close the V2/V3 global leader-economy defects and inference/eligibility defects found during Legacy+ integration: the actual place-1 leader uses the reachable 5-Moral conversion, round 10 attacks unless an individual character situation chooses Block, known LeCrisp/Толя/ScamRat removes both impossible groupmates, submitted/internal hypotheses stay synchronized, and fair Kira cannot target Sakura. The current open set remains **M171, M195, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M244, M246 and m77 close the standalone Clash through-gap attack, fallen-cell priority and opposing-facing defects. M245 removes remote-kill teleport and ranged default marching, and adds adjacent melee marching in both world directions; it remains open only for opposing claims on the same empty cell and ranged-only disjoint-column endings. The current open set is **M171, M195, M245, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

M248 closes the remaining empty-reward Ziggurat path: attacked enemies remain the primary learning pool, while an empty eligible result falls back to the global Standalone catalog and construction cost cannot commit before a reward exists. The current open set remains **M171, M195, M245, m12, m19, m24, m26**.

## Verification addendum (second pass, 2026-07-01)

A full re-verification was run over these docs: every file:line anchor (~230) was mechanically dumped and compared against the cited code — **all anchors correct**; the eight highest-risk claim clusters (agent-sourced or never personally read in the first pass) were re-read at the source; and a completeness diff was run (every `PassiveName` in characters.json and every `case` label in CharacterPassives.cs vs the docs).

**Corrected during verification** (all fixes applied in place):
1. `SecureRandom` was described as a crypto RNG in ARCHITECTURE §9 — it is a plain `System.Random` wrapper (→ new finding m21).
2. Tier semantics: Sakura and unknown_bug (Tier −1) were described as non-rolling specials — they are **secret rollable** characters (baseline range 40, humans only); unknown_bug is now additionally removed from every prediction/catalog surface. Молодой Глеб's exclusion comes from the `CharactersPull` Tier ≥ −1 filter, not a range-0 roll (m6, D3, CHARACTERS.md, GAME-DESIGN §11).
3. D5 ("2kxaoc") described the masking backwards — the passive is *exempt from* enemy-passive-name masking, not hidden by it.
4. CHARACTERS.md under-documented three verified mechanics, now added: Спартанец's "Это привилегия - умереть от моей руки" per-win rider (+1 extra Justice to the victim, −1 Int to himself, `CP:2859-2867`); Вампур's Гематофагия Psyche-priority rule (`CP:2904-2919`); Молодой Глеб's "Следит за игрой" marks up to **3** targets (`CP:4740-4780`).
5. Two flavor-only hidden passives were missing entirely: "God Of War" (Кратос) and "Искусство" (mylorik/Спартанец) — added.
6. Exact passive-name hygiene: "Стримснайпят и банят и банят и банят" and "Это привилегия - умереть от моей руки" appeared with typographic substitutions (…/—) in places; fixed to exact strings (they are load-bearing identifiers).

**Confirmed unchanged** (spot-listed because they were the highest hallucination risks): all `Luck()` probability figures (Luck(a,b) ≈ a-in-b, `SecureRandom.cs:35-45`); m19 (Itachi crows/Izanagi truly absent from the web layer); M2 (Jew widget state source); the bot moral thresholds 20/13/8/5/3 and round>10 auto-block; the AdminPlayerType runtime injection (`GameUpdateMess.cs:253`); the ARCHITECTURE §3 handler line table (all rows). No finding was retracted; C1–M12 all stand.

## Doc sweep — designer verdicts on ambiguous findings (2026-07-03)

The designer reviewed every ambiguous finding in `docs/DESIGNER-REVIEW.md`. The **БАГ** ones were fixed (each carries a `**Fixed:**` note above). The following were confirmed **ОК / intended** — no code change; the ⚠ divergence marks in `docs/CHARACTERS.md` and `docs/INTERACTION-MATRIX.md` were converted to "intended".

| Finding | Verdict (ОК) as confirmed by the designer |
|---|---|
| **M3** | AWDKA silently forced to last place before every fight calculation — intended hidden mechanic. |
| **M5** | "Тигр топ" opening window at game start (rounds 1–3) — intended, in addition to the later random window. |
| **M6** | "Лучше с двумя" counts Тигр himself — intended: Тигр is a member of his own clan. |
| **M12** | Монстр's "Пейзаж конца света" **does** kill goblin pawns — intended (the one kill-source goblins aren't immune to). |
| **m8** | Толя "Подсчет" cd 4–5 — no bug; the description counts from the **end of the effect**. |
| **m9** | Итачи Цукуеми recharge 4 rounds — no bug; changed nothing. |
| **m13** | HardKitty's opening −30 **score** logged as "−30 Морали" — intentional text joke. |
| **m14** | Butcher sup marks only from round 2 — intended. |
| **D1** | ~~Darksci can dodge Дизмораль by hoarding the round-9 level-up — intended tech.~~ **Reversed 2026-07-03**: web-only banking artifact; closed by M15 (web level-up gate). Darksci now eats the −5 on both platforms. |
| **D4** | "Булинг" / "Го играть" / "lvl-мяк" / "2kxaoc" keyed on character Name — intended (name-specific jokes). |
| **D5** | "2kxaoc" is a visible-only meme (exempt from enemy-passive masking) — intended. |
| **D6** | Вампуризм **copies** the victim's Justice (doesn't drain) — intended. |
| **D8** | "Пейзаж конца света" +7 **regular** (×4 = +28) — intended; changed nothing. |
| **D9** | Premade **copies** the Carry's fight-moral (Carry keeps theirs) — intended. |
| **D10** | Ziggurat copies any `Standalone` passive incl. Булькает / dead copies — left as-is (only «Еврей» excluded, D2). |

(**D7** was *not* ОК — the designer reclassified it as БАГ in chat; fixed in the goblin batch. See D7's `**Fixed:**` note.)
